## Multi-Sector Air Pollutants Regulations {#multi-sector-air-pollutants-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2016-151
:::

[CANADIAN ENVIRONMENTAL PROTECTION ACT, 1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31)

Registration 2016-06-17

Multi-Sector Air Pollutants Regulations

P.C. 2016-567 2016-06-17
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Whereas, pursuant to subsection 332(1)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#footnotea_e){#footnotea_e-ID0EICDA} of the
[Canadian Environmental Protection Act,
1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#footnoteb_e){#footnoteb_e-ID0EFCDA}, the Minister
of the Environment published in the [Canada
Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/), Part I, on June 7, 2014 a copy of
the proposed Multi-Sector Air Pollutants Regulations, substantially in
the annexed form, and persons were given an opportunity to file comments
with respect to the proposed Regulations or to file a notice of
objection requesting that a board of review be established and stating
the reasons for the objection;

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#footnotea_e-ID0EICDA){#footnotea_e}S.C. 2004, c.
  15, s. 31
  :::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#footnoteb_e-ID0EFCDA){#footnoteb_e}S.C. 1999, c.
  33
  :::

Whereas, pursuant to subsection 93(3) of that Act, the National Advisory
Committee has been given an opportunity to provide its advice under
section 6[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#footnotec_e){#footnotec_e-ID0EBBCA} of that Act;

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#footnotec_e-ID0EBBCA){#footnotec_e}S.C. 2015, c.
  3, par. 172(d)
  :::

And whereas, in accordance with subsection 93(4) of that Act, the
Governor in Council is of the opinion that the proposed Regulations do
not regulate an aspect of a substance that is regulated by or under any
other Act of Parliament in a manner that provides, in the opinion of the
Governor in Council, sufficient protection to the environment and human
health;

Therefore, His Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the
recommendation of the Minister of the Environment and the Minister of
Health, pursuant to subsections 93(1) and 330(3.2)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^d^](#footnoted_e){#footnoted_e-ID0EGBAA} of the
[Canadian Environmental Protection Act,
1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#footnoteb_e){#footnoteb_e-ID0EDBAA}, makes the
annexed Multi-Sector Air Pollutants Regulations.

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^d^](#footnoted_e-ID0EGBAA){#footnoted_e}S.C. 2008, c.
  31, s. 5
  :::
:::

## [Overview]{.HTitleText1} {#h-827967 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parts 1, 2 and 3

- **[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purpose of protecting the environment and human health, Parts 1, 2 and
  3 of these Regulations establish, respectively, requirements for the
  emission of the following air pollutants:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} NO~x~ from boilers and heaters in certain regulated
    facilities in various industrial sectors;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} NO~x~ from stationary spark-ignition engines that
    combust gaseous fuels in certain regulated facilities in various
    industrial sectors; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} NO~x~ and SO~2~ from cement manufacturing
    facilities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part 4 --- General

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Part 4 sets out general rules related to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the CEMS Reference Method that governs the use of a
    Continuous Emissions Monitoring System;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} alternative rules to those set out in documents
    incorporated by reference into these Regulations; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the reporting, providing, recording and retention
    of information.

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-827979 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in these Regulations.

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Canadian Environmental
      Protection Act, 1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Alberta CEMS Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Alberta CEMS Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method
      entitled Continuous Emission Monitoring System (CEMS) Code (Pub.
      No.: Ref. 107) --- published in May 1998 by Her Majesty the Queen
      in right of Alberta, as represented by the Minister responsible
      for Alberta Environmental Protection --- as read in accordance
      with subsection (4). ([Code SMECE de l'Alberta]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[alumina facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[alumina facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility that is
      used or designed to produce alumina from bauxite for use in the
      production of aluminum. ([installation de production
      d'alumine]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[aluminum facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[aluminum facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility that is
      used or designed

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to produce aluminum from alumina;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to produce prebaked anodes for use in the
        production of aluminum; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to calcinate petroleum coke for use in the
        production of aluminum. ([aluminerie]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[asphalt refinery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[asphalt refinery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility ---
      other than a petroleum refinery --- at which the annual volume of
      asphalt produced is greater than 33% of the annual volume of
      liquid petroleum products produced and that is used or designed to
      process, using distillation,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} crude oil or bitumen;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} blends of crude oil, or bitumen, with other
        hydrocarbon compounds; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} partially refined feedstock derived from crude
        oil or bitumen. ([raffinerie d'asphalte]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[ASTM]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ASTM]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means ASTM International, formerly
      known as the American Society for Testing and
      Materials. ([ASTM]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ASTM D6522-11]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ASTM D6522-11]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method entitled
      Standard Test Method for Determination of Nitrogen Oxides, Carbon
      Monoxide, and Oxygen Concentrations in Emissions from Natural
      Gas-Fired Reciprocating Engines, Combustion Turbines, Boilers, and
      Process Heaters Using Portable Analyzers, published by
      ASTM. ([méthode ASTM D6522-11]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[authorized official]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[authorized official]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a responsible person who is an
        individual, that individual or another individual who is
        authorized to act on their behalf;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a responsible person that is a
        corporation, an officer of the corporation who is authorized to
        act on its behalf; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a responsible person that is
        another entity, an individual who is authorized to act on its
        behalf. ([agent autorisé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[base metals facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[base metals facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      pyrometallurgical or hydrometallurgical facility that is used or
      designed to recover or refine at least one of the following metals
      from feedstock that comes primarily from ore:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} nickel;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} copper;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} zinc;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} lead;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} cobalt; and

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} chromium. ([installation de production de
        métaux communs]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means combustion equipment ---
      other than combustion equipment that is used only in the
      production of electricity for sale --- that is used or designed to
      transfer thermal energy from the combustion of a fuel to water or
      steam or another fluid. ([chaudière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[cement manufacturing facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cement manufacturing facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      facility that is used or designed to produce
      clinker. ([cimenterie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CEMS Reference Method]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CEMS Reference Method]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the EC CEMS
      Code or the Alberta CEMS Code. ([méthode de référence des
      SMECE]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CFR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CFR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means Title 40, Chapter I of the Code
      of Federal Regulations of the United
      States. ([CFR]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[chemicals facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[chemicals facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility that
      is used or designed to manufacture chemical substances from
      feedstock as its primary activity and where at least one of the
      following substances is manufactured:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} adipic acid, esters of adipic acid, or amines
        of adipic acid;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} titanium dioxide;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} carbon black;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} butyl rubber;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} ethylene that is produced from refined
        petroleum, liquid hydrocarbons or natural gas;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} ethylene glycol;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} grain ethanol for use in industrial
        applications or as fuel;

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} linear alpha olefins;

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} ethylene-based polymers;

      - [(j)]{.lawlabel} methanol;

      - [(k)]{.lawlabel} iso-octane;

      - [(l)]{.lawlabel} hydrogen that is produced, primarily for sale,
        from steam reforming;

      - [(m)]{.lawlabel} linear alkyl benzene;

      - [(n)]{.lawlabel} terephthalic acid;

      - [(o)]{.lawlabel} paraxylene;

      - [(p)]{.lawlabel} styrene or polystyrene;

      - [(q)]{.lawlabel} sodium hydroxide;

      - [(r)]{.lawlabel} citric acid;

      - [(s)]{.lawlabel} nylon resins, fibres and filaments; and

      - [(t)]{.lawlabel} nitric acid. ([installation de fabrication de
        produits chimiques]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Continuous Emission Monitoring System]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Continuous Emission Monitoring System]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[CEMS]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means equipment for the sampling,
      conditioning and analyzing of emissions from a given source and
      the recording of data related to those emissions. ([système de
      mesure et d'enregistrement en continu des
      émissions]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [SMECE]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[diesel fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[diesel fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fuel that can
      evaporate at atmospheric pressure, that boils at a temperature of
      at least 130ºC and at most 400ºC and that is for use in diesel
      engines. ([carburant diesel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EC CEMS Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EC CEMS Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method entitled
      Protocols and Performance Specifications for Continuous Monitoring
      of Gaseous Emissions from Thermal Power Generation (EPS 1/PG/7)
      --- published with revisions in December 2005 by Her Majesty the
      Queen in right of Canada, as represented by the Minister --- as
      read in accordance with subsection (3). ([Code SMECE
      d'EC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EC Method A]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EC Method A]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method known as
      Method A: Determination of Sampling Site and Traverse Points in
      Reference Method EPS 1/RM/8. ([méthode A de l'EC]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[EC Method B]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EC Method B]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method known as
      Method B: Determination of Stack Gas Velocity and Volumetric Flow
      Rate in Reference Method EPS 1/RM/8. ([méthode B de
      l'EC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EC Method D]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EC Method D]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method known as
      Method D: Determination of Moisture Content in Reference Method
      EPS 1/RM/8. ([méthode D de l'EC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an engine that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} when used, is stationary and is not in or on a
        machine that is self-propelled;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} operates under characteristics significantly
        similar to the theoretical Otto combustion cycle; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} uses a spark plug or other sparking
        device. ([moteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EPA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EPA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Environmental Protection
      Agency of the United States. ([EPA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EPA Method 1]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EPA Method 1]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method entitled
      Method 1 --- Sample and Velocity Traverses for Stationary Sources,
      set out in Appendix A-1 to Part 60 of the CFR. ([méthode 1 de
      l'EPA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EPA Method 1A]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EPA Method 1A]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method entitled
      Method 1A --- Sample and Velocity Traverses for Stationary Sources
      With Small Stacks or Ducts, set out in Appendix A-1 to Part 60 of
      the CFR. ([méthode 1A de l'EPA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EPA Method 2]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EPA Method 2]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method entitled
      Method 2 --- Determination of Stack Gas Velocity and Volumetric
      Flow Rate (Type S pitot tube), set out in Appendix A-1 to Part 60
      of the CFR. ([méthode 2 de l'EPA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EPA Method 3A]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EPA Method 3A]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method entitled
      Method 3A --- Determination of Oxygen and Carbon Dioxide
      Concentrations in Emissions From Stationary Sources (Instrumental
      Analyzer Procedure), set out in Appendix A-2 to Part 60 of the
      CFR. ([méthode 3A de l'EPA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EPA Method 4]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EPA Method 4]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method entitled
      Method 4 --- Determination of Moisture Content in Stack Gases, set
      out in Appendix A-3 to Part 60 of the CFR. ([méthode 4 de
      l'EPA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EPA Method 6C]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EPA Method 6C]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method entitled
      Method 6C --- Determination of Sulfur Dioxide Emissions From
      Stationary Sources (Instrumental Analyzer Procedure), set out in
      Appendix A-4 to Part 60 of the CFR. ([méthode 6C de
      l'EPA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EPA Method 7E]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EPA Method 7E]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method entitled
      Method 7E --- Determination of Nitrogen Oxides Emissions from
      Stationary Sources (Instrumental Analyzer Procedure), set out in
      Appendix A-4 to Part 60 of the CFR. ([méthode 7E de
      l'EPA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the buildings, other
      structures and stationary equipment that are located on a single
      site or on adjacent sites that function as a single integrated
      site. ([installation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[gaseous fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gaseous fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fuel that is gaseous
      at a temperature of 15.6ºC and an absolute pressure of 101.325
      kPa. ([combustible gazeux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a petroleum distillate, or
      a blend of petroleum distillates, oxygenates or additives, that is
      suitable for use in a spark-ignition engine and that has the
      following characteristics, as determined by the applicable test
      method listed in the National Standard of Canada
      CAN/CGSB-3.5-2011, Automotive Gasoline:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a vapour pressure of at least 35 kPa;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an antiknock index of at least 80;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a distillation temperature, at which 10% of the
        fuel evaporates, of at least 35°C and at most 70°C; and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a distillation temperature, at which 50% of the
        fuel evaporates, of at least 60°C and at most
        120°C. ([essence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[heater]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[heater]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means combustion equipment, other
      than a boiler, that is used or designed to transfer thermal energy
      from the combustion of a fuel to a material that is being
      processed outside the combustion chamber. ([four
      industriel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[iron ore pelletizing facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[iron ore pelletizing facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      facility that is used or designed to produce iron ore pellets from
      iron ore concentrate using an induration furnace. ([installation
      de bouletage du minerai de fer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[iron, steel and ilmenite facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[iron, steel and ilmenite facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      facility --- other than a foundry that produces iron or steel
      castings --- that is used or designed to produce at least one of
      the following products:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} metallurgical coke from coal;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} titanium slag or iron from iron- or
        titanium-bearing ores or from iron ore pellets; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} steel from iron or scrap steel. ([installation
        de production de fer, d'acier et d'ilménite]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[liquid petroleum product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[liquid petroleum product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} naphtha;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} gasoline;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} aviation turbine fuel;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} kerosene;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} diesel fuel;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} light fuel oil;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} heavy fuel oil;

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} naval distillate, bunker fuel or any other
        marine fuel;

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} gas oil;

      - [(j)]{.lawlabel} lubricant basestock or petroleum-based
        lubricant;

      - [(k)]{.lawlabel} asphalt; or

      - [(l)]{.lawlabel} synthetic crude oil. ([produit pétrolier
        liquide]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[nitrogen-based fertilizer facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[nitrogen-based fertilizer facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      facility that is used or designed to manufacture at least one of
      the following substances:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} anhydrous ammonia, or aqueous ammonia, produced
        from steam methane reforming; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} urea. ([installation de fabrication d'engrais à
        base d'azote]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[NO~x~]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[NO~x~]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means oxides of nitrogen, which is
      the sum of nitric oxide (NO) and nitrogen dioxide
      (NO~2~). ([NO~x~]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[oil and gas facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[oil and gas facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility,
      including an asphalt refinery or underground storage facility for
      gaseous fuel, that is used or designed

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to extract hydrocarbons from underground
        deposits or reservoirs other than by means of thermal methods or
        surface mining;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to transport or process those hydrocarbons;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to transport or treat wastewater or waste that
        is related to the extraction or processing of those hydrocarbons
        for its injection underground; or

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to inject that wastewater or waste underground.

      It does not include an oil sands facility, petroleum refinery,
      chemicals facility, nitrogen-based fertilizer facility or facility
      --- other than an underground storage facility for gaseous fuel
      --- that is primarily engaged in the local distribution of natural
      gas. ([installation d'exploitation pétrolière et
      gazière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[oil sands facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[oil sands facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility ---
      other than a chemicals facility, nitrogen-based fertilizer
      facility or asphalt refinery --- that is used or designed to
      engage in at least one of the following activities:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the surface mining of crude oil- or
        bitumen-containing sand from geological deposits;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the processing of that sand to separate crude
        oil or bitumen from that sand;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the extraction of crude oil or bitumen from
        underground deposits or reservoirs by means of thermal methods;
        and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the upgrading by means of the processing, using
        distillation, of crude oil or bitumen, or of blends of crude
        oil, or bitumen, with other hydrocarbon compounds, to produce a
        combined annual volume of gasoline, diesel fuel and lubricant
        basestock that is at most 40% of the facility's annual volume of
        liquid petroleum products produced. ([installation
        d'exploitation de sables bitumineux]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who has the
      charge, management or control of a boiler, heater, engine or
      cement manufacturing facility. ([exploitant]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[petroleum refinery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[petroleum refinery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility at
      which the combined annual volume of gasoline, diesel fuel and
      lubricant basestock produced is greater than 40% of the annual
      volume of liquid petroleum products produced and that is used or
      designed to process, using distillation,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} crude oil or bitumen;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} blends of crude oil, or bitumen, with other
        hydrocarbon compounds; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} partially refined feedstock derived from crude
        oil or bitumen. ([raffinerie de pétrole]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[potash facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[potash facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility that is
      used or designed to produce potash, including a facility that is
      used or designed to extract potash-bearing ore. ([installation de
      production de potasse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[power plant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[power plant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility that is used
      or designed to produce electricity for sale via the electric grid
      as its primary activity. ([centrale électrique]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[ppmvd]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ppmvd]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means parts per million, by volume
      on a dry basis. ([ppmvs]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[pulp and paper facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pulp and paper facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility
      that is used or designed to produce

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} pulp from wood or from other plant material or
        paper products; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any product from pulp or a pulping
        process. ([installation de production de pâte et
        papier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Reference Method EPS 1/RM/8]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Reference Method EPS 1/RM/8]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      document entitled Reference Method for Source Testing: Measurement
      of Releases of Particulate from Stationary Sources, published in
      December 1993 by Her Majesty the Queen in right of Canada, as
      represented by the Minister. ([Méthode de référence SPE
      1/RM/8]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[responsible person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[responsible person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an owner or
      operator of a boiler or heater, an engine or a cement
      manufacturing facility. ([personne responsable]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[SO~2~]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[SO~2~]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means sulphur
      dioxide. ([SO~2~]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[thermal method]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[thermal method]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a method of crude oil
      or bitumen extraction that involves the introduction of thermal
      energy into a geological formation in order to enhance the
      fluidity of crude oil or bitumen and to facilitate its extraction.
      It includes steam-assisted gravity drainage, cyclic steam
      stimulation, Toe-to-Heel Air Injection (THAI^®^), [in
      situ]{.otherLang lang="la"} combustion, flooding with heated
      water, solvent-assisted thermal methods and electro-thermal
      methods. ([méthode thermique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[underground storage facility for gaseous fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[underground storage facility for gaseous
      fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility --- other than an oil
      sands facility, petrol refinery or asphalt facility --- that is
      used or designed to store gaseous fuel underground. ([installation
      de stockage souterrain de combustibles gazeux]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a calendar
      year. ([année]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation of documents
  incorporated by reference

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of interpreting any document that is
  incorporated by reference into these Regulations, "should" must be
  read to mean "must" and any recommendation or suggestion must be read
  as an obligation, unless the context requires otherwise. For greater
  certainty, the context of the accuracy or precision of a measurement
  can never require otherwise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}EC CEMS Code

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The EC CEMS Code is to be read as set out in Schedule
  1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alberta CEMS Code

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Alberta CEMS Code is to be read as set out in
  Schedule 2.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}EPA discretion

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Any EPA method that is incorporated by reference into
  these Regulations must be read without reference to the exercise of
  discretion by the EPA or by the Administrator of the EPA.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inconsistency

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In the event of an inconsistency between a provision
  of these Regulations and a document that is incorporated by reference
  into these Regulations, the provision prevails to the extent of the
  inconsistency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Methods incorporated by reference

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Any method of the EPA or ASTM that is incorporated by
  reference into these Regulations is incorporated as amended from time
  to time.

## [Responsibility]{.HTitleText1} {#h-828120 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Who must comply

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** Unless the context requires
that a particular responsible person complies, a requirement set out in
these Regulations in respect of, respectively, a boiler or heater, an
engine or a cement manufacturing facility, including a requirement in
respect of a kiln located in it, must be complied with by a responsible
person for the boiler or heater, engine or cement manufacturing
facility.

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Boilers and Heaters]{.HTitleText1} {#h-828123 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-828124 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** The following definitions
apply in this Part and in Schedules 3 to 7.

[[alternative gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[alternative gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a gaseous fossil fuel
    other than natural gas. ([gaz de remplacement]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[amendment day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[amendment day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the day on which the
    Regulations Amending the Multi-Sector Air Pollutants Regulations
    (Part 1 --- Biomass) come into force. ([date de
    modification]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[anode-baking furnace]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[anode-baking furnace]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a heater that
    bakes green anodes to produce blocks of carbon for use in the
    production of aluminum. ([four de cuisson d'anodes]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[ASTM D1945-03]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ASTM D1945-03]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method entitled
    Standard Test Method for Analysis of Natural Gas by Gas
    Chromatography, published by ASTM. ([méthode ASTM
    D1945-03]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[ASTM D1946-90]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ASTM D1946-90]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the method entitled
    Standard Practice for Analysis of Reformed Gas by Gas
    Chromatography, published by ASTM. ([méthode ASTM
    D1946-90]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[biomass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[biomass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a liquid or solid fuel that
    consists only of non-fossilized organic material that originates
    from plants or animals but does not come from a geological
    formation, including

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} liquid and solid products or waste produced from
      that material;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} liquids and solids recovered from that material,
      including from organic waste; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} sludge from wastewater
      treatment. ([biomasse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[biomass boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[biomass boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a boiler that can reach at least 50% of its rated
      capacity when it is in a steady state and at least 90% of the
      input energy in its combustion chamber results from the
      introduction of biomass; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a boiler that is commissioned or
      recommissioned on or after amendment day, a boiler that is linked
      to equipment that is capable of introducing a sufficient quantity
      of biomass as the input energy in its combustion chamber to allow
      it to meet the criteria set out in paragraph (a). ([chaudière à
      biomasse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[biomass heater]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[biomass heater]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a heater that can reach at least 50% of its rated
      capacity when it is in a steady state and at least 90% of the
      input energy in its combustion chamber results from the
      introduction of biomass; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a heater that is commissioned or
      recommissioned on or after amendment day, a heater that is linked
      to equipment that is capable of introducing a sufficient quantity
      of biomass as the input energy in its combustion chamber to allow
      it to meet the criteria set out in paragraph (a). ([four
      industriel à biomasse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[blast furnace stove]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[blast furnace stove]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vertical
    cylindrical regenerator filled with refractory and used to preheat
    ambient air that is then introduced into a blast furnace used in
    ironmaking. ([récupérateur de haut fourneau]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[CEMS test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[CEMS test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a determination, by means of
    a CEMS in accordance with section 32, of the NO~x~ emission
    intensity of a boiler or heater. ([essai SMECE]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[chemical recovery boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[chemical recovery boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a boiler
    whose fuel includes spent pulping liquor and that recovers chemical
    constituents from the combustion of that spent pulping
    liquor. ([chaudière de récupération chimique]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[class 40]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[class 40]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a pre-existing
    boiler or heater, describes a class 40 boiler or heater within the
    meaning of section 12. ([classe 40]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[class 70]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[class 70]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a pre-existing
    boiler or heater, describes a class 70 boiler or heater within the
    meaning of section 12. ([classe 70]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[class 80]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[class 80]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a pre-existing
    boiler or heater, describes a class 80 boiler or heater within the
    meaning of section 12. ([classe 80]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[coke oven]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[coke oven]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an oven that converts coal
    to coke through distillation. ([four à coke]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[coke oven battery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[coke oven battery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a heater that
    consists of a combustion chamber, with more than one burner, whose
    exhaust gas circulate between coke ovens. ([batterie de fours à
    coke]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[commercial grade natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[commercial grade natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means natural
    gas that is supplied by a commercial supplier. ([gaz naturel de
    qualité commerciale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[commissioning date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[commissioning date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the day on which a
    boiler or heater begins to produce thermal energy primarily for use
    in production or to provide heat. ([date de mise en
    service]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[ethylene cracker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ethylene cracker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a heater that
    transforms a mixture of steam and hydrocarbons into hydrocarbon
    gases, notably ethylene. ([craqueur d'éthylène]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[gaseous fossil fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[gaseous fossil fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes gaseous fossil
    fuel that is a by-product of an industrial process, or industrial
    operation, and that has constituents with thermal energy
    value. ([combustible fossile gazeux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[heat-recovery steam generator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[heat-recovery steam generator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
    equipment that captures useful thermal energy from the hot exhaust
    gas of a gas turbine, or from a set of reciprocating engines, to
    produce steam. ([générateur de vapeur à récupération de
    chaleur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[modern]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[modern]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a boiler or heater,
    describes a boiler or heater that is not transitional whose
    commissioning date is on or after the day on which these Regulations
    are registered. ([moderne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a gaseous fossil fuel that
    consists of at least 90% methane by volume. ([gaz
    naturel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[NO~x~ emission intensity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[NO~x~ emission intensity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the quantity
    of NO~x~ emitted by a boiler or heater, expressed in grams of NO~x~
    emitted per gigajoule of thermal energy in the fuel (g/GJ), based on
    the higher heating value of the fuel combusted. ([intensité
    d'émission de NO~x~]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[packaged]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[packaged]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a boiler or heater,
    describes a boiler or heater that is received at the facility in a
    state of near-complete assembly and that requires, at the facility,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} assembling any prefabricated components;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fixing it to its location; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} making the connections necessary for its
      operation. ([préfabriqué]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[pre-existing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[pre-existing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a boiler or
    heater, describes a boiler or heater whose commissioning date is
    before the day on which these Regulations are
    registered. ([préexistant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[preheated air]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[preheated air]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means air that is preheated
    above ambient air temperature before it is introduced into the
    combustion chamber of a boiler or heater. ([air
    préchauffé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[rated capacity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[rated capacity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a boiler or
    heater, means the maximum thermal energy --- based on the higher
    heating value of the fuel combusted --- that the boiler or heater
    is, on its commissioning date, capable of producing in an hour,
    expressed in GJ/h, as specified on the nameplate affixed to the
    boiler or heater by its manufacturer or, in the absence of such a
    nameplate, as set out in a document provided by the
    manufacturer. ([capacité nominale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[recommissioning date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[recommissioning date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the day on which
    a boiler or heater begins to produce thermal energy primarily for
    use in production or to provide heat after

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a major modification referred to in subsection
      13(2); or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a redesign referred to in paragraph
      10(2)(b). ([date de remise en service]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[reheat furnace]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[reheat furnace]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a heater in which steel
    is reheated for hot rolling into basic shapes. ([four de
    réchauffage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[stack test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[stack test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a determination, in
    accordance with sections 27 to 31, of the NO~x~ emission intensity
    of a boiler or heater. ([essai en cheminée]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[standard m^3^]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[standard m^3^]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned to a
    cubic metre at standard pressure and standard temperature by the
    definition [[standard volume]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection
    2(1) of the [Electricity and Gas Inspection
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-86-131). ([m^3^
    normalisé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[steady state]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[steady state]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an operating state that
    is other than start-up, shutdown or upset. ([état
    stable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[steam methane reformer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[steam methane reformer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a heater that
    transforms a mixture of steam and hydrocarbons in the presence of a
    catalyst to produce hydrogen and carbon oxides and includes --- if
    it shares a common stack with the heater --- any integrated
    auxiliary boiler that is used to produce that steam or other
    integrated equipment that heats that steam. ([reformeur de méthane à
    la vapeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[transitional]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[transitional]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a boiler or
    heater, describes a boiler or heater that is located in a regulated
    facility set out in subsection 5(2) on its commissioning date and
    whose commissioning date occurs in the period that begins on the day
    on which these Regulations are registered and ends

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the boiler or heater is packaged, three months
      after that day; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, 36 months after that day. ([de
      transition]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-828170 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rated capacity at least 10.5 GJ/h

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  Part applies in respect of a pre-existing, transitional or modern
  boiler or heater located in a regulated facility that is used or
  designed to combust gaseous fossil fuel and that has a rated capacity
  of at least 10.5 GJ/h.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulated facilities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following are the regulated facilities:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} oil and gas facilities;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} oil sands facilities;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} chemicals facilities;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} nitrogen-based fertilizer facilities;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} pulp and paper facilities;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} base metals facilities;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} potash facilities;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} alumina facilities and aluminum facilities;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} power plants;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} iron, steel and ilmenite facilities;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} iron ore pelletizing facilities; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} cement manufacturing facilities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded boilers and heaters

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (1) and (2), this Part does not
  apply in respect of the following types of boiler or heater:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a heater that is used to dry, bake or calcinate
    materials, including a kiln as defined in section 101 and an
    anode-baking furnace;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a heater that is used in any process to chemically
    transform ore or intermediate products into bulk metallic products;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a coke oven battery;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a heater or boiler that is designed to combust coke
    oven gas;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a blast furnace stove;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a heater or boiler that is designed to combust
    blast furnace gas;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} an ethylene cracker;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a steam methane reformer;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a reheat furnace;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a boiler or heater that is used only for activities
    that are subsequent to the hot rolling of steel into basic shapes at
    an iron, steel and ilmenite facility;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a chemical recovery boiler;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} a biomass boiler or biomass heater;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} a heat-recovery steam generator;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} a boiler that combusts exhaust gases that arise
    from the partial combustion of coke in a vessel integrated with a
    fluid coking unit; and

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} a boiler or heater that is used only in the
    start-up of a facility or process and operated for fewer than 500
    hours in each previous year of its operation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Percentage of input energy

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of paragraph (3)(l), the percentage
  of the input energy in a biomass boiler's or biomass heater's
  combustion chamber resulting from the introduction of biomass is to be
  determined, for a given hour while the boiler or heater is in a steady
  state, by the formula

  ::: {#1260326 .Subsection}
  E~bio~/(E~bio~ + E~cng~ + E~gff~ + E~o~ + E~s~) × 100

  where

  [E~bio~]{.dfn}
  :   is the input energy resulting from the introduction of biomass for
      the given hour, determined by the formula
      ::: {#1260329 .NestedFormula}
      Q~bio~ × HHV~bio~

      where

      [Q~bio~]{.dfn}
      :   is the quantity of the biomass combusted during the given
          hour, as measured by a flow meter on the input, expressed in
          kl for liquid biomass and in t for solid biomass, and

      [HHV~bio~]{.dfn}
      :   is the higher heating value of the biomass combusted during
          the given hour, expressed in GJ/kl for liquid biomass and in
          GJ/t for solid biomass, being
          - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the higher heating value determined in
            accordance with any of the required HHV methods set out in
            section 22 that apply, or

          - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the default higher heating value set out in
            column 2 of the applicable table to Schedule 3 for the type
            of fuel set out in column 1;
      :::

  [E~cng~]{.dfn}
  :   is the input energy resulting from the introduction of commercial
      grade natural gas for the given hour, determined by the formula
      ::: {#1260336 .NestedFormula}
      Q~cng~ × HHV~cng~

      where

      [Q~cng~]{.dfn}
      :   is the quantity of the commercial grade natural gas combusted
          during the given hour, as measured by a flow meter on the
          input, expressed in standard m^3^, and

      [HHV~cng~]{.dfn}
      :   is the higher heating value of the commercial grade natural
          gas combusted during the given hour, expressed in GJ/standard
          m^3^, being
          - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the higher heating value determined in
            accordance with any of the required HHV methods set out in
            section 22 that apply, or

          - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 0.03793;
      :::

  [E~gff~]{.dfn}
  :   is the input energy resulting from the introduction of gaseous
      fossil fuel, other than commercial grade natural gas, for the
      given hour, determined by the formula
      ::: {#1260343 .NestedFormula}
      Q~gff~ × HHV~gff~

      where

      [Q~gff~]{.dfn}
      :   is the quantity of the gaseous fossil fuel, other than
          commercial grade natural gas, combusted during the given hour,
          as measured by a flow meter on the input, expressed in
          standard m^3^, and

      [HHV~gff~]{.dfn}
      :   is the higher heating value of the gaseous fossil fuel, other
          than commercial grade natural gas, combusted during the given
          hour, expressed in GJ/standard m^3^, determined in accordance
          with any of the required HHV methods set out in section 22
          that apply;
      :::

  [E~o~]{.dfn}
  :   is the input energy resulting from the introduction of a fuel
      other than biomass or gaseous fossil fuel during the given hour,
      determined by the formula
      ::: {#1260348 .NestedFormula}
      Σ~i~(Q~i~ × HHV~i~)

      where

      [Q~i~]{.dfn}
      :   is the quantity of the ith fuel, other than gaseous fossil
          fuel, combusted during the given hour, as measured by a flow
          meter on the input, expressed in standard m^3^ for gaseous
          fuels, in kl for liquid fuels and in t for solid fuels,

      [HHV~i~]{.dfn}
      :   is the higher heating value of the ith fuel, other than
          gaseous fossil fuel, combusted during the given hour,
          expressed in GJ/standard m^3^ for gaseous fuels, in GJ/kl for
          liquid fuels and in GJ/t for solid fuels, being
          - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the higher heating value determined in
            accordance with any of the required HHV methods set out in
            section 22 that apply, or

          - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the default higher heating value set out in
            column 2 of the applicable table to Schedule 3 for the type
            of fuel set out in column 1, and

      [i]{.dfn}
      :   is the ith fuel, other than biomass or gaseous fossil fuel,
          combusted, where i goes from 1 to n and where n is the number
          of those fuels combusted; and
      :::

  [E~s~]{.dfn}
  :   is the input energy, expressed in GJ, that originates from a
      source other than the combustion of fuel in the boiler's or
      heater's combustion chamber during the given hour, determined in
      accordance with generally accepted engineering principles.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for information

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A responsible person for a biomass boiler or biomass
  heater must, on the Minister's request, provide to the Minister any of
  the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} information and supporting documents obtained from
    the manufacturer of the boiler or heater that demonstrates that it
    is a biomass boiler or biomass heater, as the case may be; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the results of a field test that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} was conducted for a period of at least one hour,
      by a person who is independent of all responsible persons for the
      boiler or heater and demonstrates that the boiler or heater is a
      biomass boiler or biomass heater, as the case may be, and that the
      percentage referred to in subsection (4) has been determined in
      accordance with that subsection, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is supported by documents in the possession of a
      responsible person for the boiler or heater that establish that
      the independent person referred to in subparagraph (i)

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} is an engineer who is, under the laws of the
        province in which the biomass boiler or biomass heater is
        located, authorized to practise engineering in relation to
        combustion equipment, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} has demonstrated knowledge of, and at least
        five years' experience as the technical lead of projects that
        involved, the design of combustion equipment.

### [Main Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-828205 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Modern boilers

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to section 10, the NO~x~ emission intensity of a modern boiler that,
  for a given hour, has at least 50% of the input energy in its
  combustion chamber resulting from the introduction of gaseous fossil
  fuel set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection and that has
  a thermal efficiency set out in column 2 must not, for that hour,
  exceed the limit set out in column 3.

  <figure id="828209">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top"></th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 3</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Gaseous Fossil
  Fuel</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Thermal
  Efficiency</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom alignleft" scope="col">NO<sub>x</sub>
  Emission Intensity Limit (g/GJ)</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr style="height:10pt">
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">Natural gas</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">&lt; 80%</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">16</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="row">Natural
  gas</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">≥ 80% and ≤ 90%</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom top">16 + (E – 80)/5, where E is the thermal
  efficiency of the boiler</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="row">Natural
  gas</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">&gt; 90%</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom top">18</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">4</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">Alternative
  gas</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">&lt; 80%</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">20.8</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">5</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">Alternative
  gas</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">≥ 80% and ≤ 90%</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">20.8 + (E – 80)/4.54, where E is the thermal
  efficiency of the boiler</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">6</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight" scope="row">Alternative gas</td>

  <p>Modern Boilers — NO<sub>X</sub> Emission Intensity
  Limits</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Thermal efficiency

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (1), the thermal
  efficiency of a modern boiler for the given hour

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if there has been a determination made in
    accordance with section 18, is the result of the most recent such
    determination; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, is deemed to be less than 80%.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Modern heaters

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to section 10, the NO~x~ emission intensity of a modern heater ---
  that, for a given hour, has at least 50% of the input energy in its
  combustion chamber resulting from the introduction of gaseous fossil
  fuel set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection and that has
  a difference between the temperature of its preheated air and the
  ambient air set out in column 2 must not, for that hour, exceed the
  limit set out in column 3.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preheated air

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (1), the difference
  between the temperature of a modern heater's preheated air and the
  ambient air, expressed in °C, for the given hour

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if there has been a determination of that
    difference made in accordance with section 24 within the previous 12
    months, is the result of the most recent of such determination; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, is deemed to be 0°C.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of type of gaseous fossil
fuel

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
determining the type of gaseous fossil fuel --- natural gas or
alternative gas --- introduced into the combustion chamber of a boiler
or heater, the percentage of methane in the fuel must be determined in
accordance with section 16.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional boilers and heaters

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** The NO~x~ emission
intensity of a transitional boiler or heater that, for a given hour, has
at least 50% of the input energy in its combustion chamber resulting
from the introduction of gaseous fossil fuel must not, for that hour,
exceed the following limit:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} 26 g/GJ, if the boiler or heater has a rated capacity
  of at least 10.5 GJ/h and at most 105 GJ/h; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 10, 40 g/GJ, if the boiler or
  heater has a rated capacity greater than 105 GJ/h.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Redesigned boilers and heaters --- NO~x~
emission intensity

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  NO~x~ emission intensity of a redesigned boiler or heater that, for a
  given hour, has at least 50% of the input energy in its combustion
  chamber resulting from the introduction of gaseous fossil fuel must
  not, for that hour, exceed the limit of 26 g/GJ.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Redesigned boilers and heaters

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A redesigned boiler or heater is one that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on its commissioning date, was not designed to
    combust gaseous fossil fuel; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after the day on which these Regulations are
    registered, is redesigned to combust, on its recommissioning date,
    gaseous fossil fuel.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Class 80 and class 70

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** The NO~x~ emission
intensity of a pre-existing boiler or heater that is class 80 or class
70 --- other than those referred to in subsections 13(1) and 14(1) and
(2) --- and that, for a given hour, has at least 50% of the input energy
in its combustion chamber resulting from the introduction of gaseous
fossil fuel must not, for that hour, exceed the limit of 26 g/GJ, as of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} January 1, 2026, for a class 80 boiler or heater; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} January 1, 2036, for a class 70 boiler or heater.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pre-existing boilers and heaters ---
classification

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  pre-existing boiler or heater is classified --- in accordance with its
  classification NO~x~ emission intensity determined in accordance with
  subsection 34(1) or redetermined in accordance with subsection 36(1)
  --- as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} class 80, if its classification NO~x~ emission
    intensity is determined to be at least 80 g/GJ;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} class 70, if its classification NO~x~ emission
    intensity is determined to be at least 70 g/GJ and less than 80
    g/GJ; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} class 40, in any other case.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Before classification --- deemed class
  80

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A pre-existing boiler or heater --- other than a
  redesigned boiler or heater referred to in subsection 10(2) --- that
  is not classified under subsection (1) is deemed to be class 80 and to
  have a classification NO~x~ emission intensity of 80 g/GJ.

  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044142-1388322.html"
  role="button"}
:::
