  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} July 1 of the year that follows the year during
    which the engine is designated as belonging to the group, if the
    designation occurs on or after January 1, 2019.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration date --- engines not in
  group

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An engine referred to in subsection (2) that does not
  belong to a group must be registered on or before

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a pre-existing engine, the later of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} January 1, 2019, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} July 1 of the year that follows the first year
      during which there is a responsible person for the engine, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a modern engine, July 1 of the year that
    follows the first year during which there is a responsible person
    for the engine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Re-registration

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An engine that has been registered in accordance with
  subsection (4) --- or re-registered under this subsection --- must be
  re-registered on or before July 1 of the year that follows the last
  year during which there remains at least one responsible person for
  the engine who has registered or re-registered the engine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The registration occurs when the Minister is provided
  with the information for inclusion in the engine registry in respect
  of the engine that is set out in Schedule 9.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of information --- engine registry

- **[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  there is a change in the information provided to the Minister for
  inclusion in the engine registry in respect of an engine or a
  replacement unit, the Minister must be provided with the updated
  information

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within 30 days after the place where a record is
    kept is changed, if the updated information involves a change in the
    place where a record is kept; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on or before July 1 of the year that follows the
    year during which the change occurred but before a compliance report
    in respect of that year is provided to the Minister, in any other
    case.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsible person

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The updated information must be provided by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the responsible person referred to in subsection
    97(3), if the updated information is in respect of an engine
    referred to in that subsection or in respect of a replacement unit
    referred to in subsection 64(4); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a responsible person who has registered or
    re-registered the engine in the engine registry in accordance with
    subsection 97(4) or (5), if the updated information is in respect of
    an engine referred to in that subsection.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance reports

- **[[99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-99 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  compliance report must, on or before July 1 of a year, be provided
  that contains the information set out in Schedule 10 in respect of the
  preceding year to the Minister in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an engine or replacement unit that, during the
    preceding year, belongs to a subgroup; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an engine that, during the preceding year,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is subject to a NO~x~ emission intensity limit,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is low-use; or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} combusts fuel for a period referred to in
      section 49.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsible person

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The compliance report must be provided by the
  responsible person for the engine or replacement unit who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provided updated information in accordance with
    paragraph 98(2)(a) in respect of an engine or replacement unit
    referred to in that paragraph, if that updated information was
    provided;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provided updated information in accordance with
    paragraph 98(2)(b) in respect of an engine referred to in that
    paragraph, if that updated information was provided; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} registered the engine in the engine registry in
    accordance with subsection 97((3) or (4) or re-registered it in the
    engine registry in accordance with subsection 97(5), in any other
    case.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record-making

**[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** In addition to any
records otherwise required under these Regulations, a record in respect
of an engine or replacement unit that contains the following information
or documents must be made:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the engine is equipped with an emission control
  system after March 31, 2020, information that establishes that the
  engine is so equipped, along with the date on which it was equipped;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the calculation of the mass flow for each period
  referred to in section 49, along with the dates on which the period
  begins and ends;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} for each low-use engine, the following information
  concerning any period of operation during an emergency referred to in
  paragraph 51(2)(e):

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the dates on which the period begins and ends,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} its duration, in hours, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an indication of whether paragraph (a) or (b), or
    both, of the definition [[emergency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} set out in
    section 45 describes that operation;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} for each pre-existing engine with a rated brake power
  of at least 250 kW that ceases to be a regular-use engine, the serial
  number --- or, if the serial number is not known or cannot be
  obtained, the unique alphanumeric identifier --- of the engine and the
  date of that cessation;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} information that establishes the date referred to in
  paragraph 56(3)(a) or 65(3)(a), subsection 66(3), paragraph 67(2)(a)
  or 77(c) or section 92 on which the responsible person referred to in
  that paragraph, subsection or section became an owner of the engine;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} for each engine designated as belonging to a group,
  the information referred to in paragraph 56(3)(a) or (b);

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} for each engine that has had its designation as
  belonging to a group cancelled under paragraph 56(4)(b), the serial
  number --- or, if the serial number is not known or cannot be
  obtained, the unique alphanumeric identifier --- of the engine and the
  date of the cancellation;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} for each pre-existing engine or replacement unit
  belonging to a subgroup, the information referred to in paragraph
  65(3)(a) or (b);

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} for each performance test referred to in clause
  66(2)(a)(i)(B) or section 77, 78, 92 or 93 that is conducted on the
  engine,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the performance test was
    conducted,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person who conducted the
    performance test and, if that person is a corporation, the name of
    the individual who conducted the performance test, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for each test run in the performance test,

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the brake power of the engine while the test run
      was conducted and the measurements and calculations used to
      determine that brake power, and

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the NO~x~ emission intensity of the engine that
      was determined for that test run and the measurements and
      calculations that were used to make that determination;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} for each emissions check that is conducted under
  section 79, 92 or 93,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the make, model and serial number of the analyzer
    that was used,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the emissions check was
    conducted,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the serial number --- or, if the serial number is
    not known or cannot be obtained, the unique alphanumeric identifier
    --- of the engine on which the emissions check was conducted,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person who conducted the emissions
    check and, if that person is a corporation, the name of the
    individual who conducted the emissions check,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} an estimate of the brake power of the engine while
    the emissions check was conducted,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} for each minute of the sampling period for the
    emissions check referred to in subsection 88(1),

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} each NO cell temperature that was recorded, and

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} each reading of an electrochemical cell's
      response to a concentration, or recording of the average of those
      responses, of O~2~, CO, NO and NO~2~ in the engine's exhaust gas,
      and

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the flow rate of the engine's exhaust gas that
    was measured by the analyzer during the emissions check;

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} for any electrochemical analyzer used to conduct a
  sequence of calibration error checks or an emissions check,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the serial number, make and model of the
    electrochemical analyzer, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a log of operations and maintenance of the
    electrochemical analyzer;

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} for each sequence of calibration error checks of an
  analyzer that are conducted under section 83,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the serial number, make and model of the analyzer,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the sequence was conducted,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the certified concentration of each zero gas and
    of each span gas for O~2~, CO, NO or NO~2~,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the stabilization periods recorded under
    subsection 83(5) for each cell's response --- following the
    introduction of a span gas for O~2~, CO, NO or NO~2~ into the
    analyzer --- to the gas to which the cell is designed to respond,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the reading of each cell's response to a
    calibration gas referred to in subsection 83(2),

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the calibration error described in subsection
    83(6), for each zero gas and span gas for, as applicable, O~2~, CO,
    NO or NO~2~,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the flow rate of each span gas that is measured
    by the analyzer during the sequence,

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the reading of the response referred to in
    subsection 84(1) for the CO cell and NO cell, and

  - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} the CO interference response that is determined
    under subsection 84(2) and the NO interference response that is
    determined under subsection 84(3);

- [(m)]{.lawlabel} if a performance test or emissions check was
  conducted on the engine under paragraph 93(a), information that
  establishes that it was not operating for each day in the period of
  its non-operation referred to in that paragraph;

- [(n)]{.lawlabel} a log of operations and maintenance of the systems
  and components related to the engine that are set out in subsection
  95(2); and

- [(o)]{.lawlabel} for each engine referred to in subsection 95(1), the
  type of equipment or method used to control the air-fuel ratio of the
  engine, and how that ratio was verified and maintained or adjusted,
  during the diverse ambient conditions in each year, so as to ensure
  that its NO~x~ emission intensity does not exceed the NO~x~ emission
  intensity limit or NO~x~ emission value referred to in section 96.

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Cement]{.HTitleText1} {#h-829531 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part and in Schedule 11.

[[cement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a powder that results from the
    grinding of clinker and the blending of the ground clinker with
    other materials. ([ciment]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[clinker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[clinker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means solid nodules that are
    produced by the pyroprocessing of feedstock in a
    kiln. ([clinker]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[feedstock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[feedstock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a ground blend of calcium
    carbonate, silica, alumina, ferrous oxide and any other material
    that is used to produce clinker. ([matière
    première]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[grey cement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[grey cement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means cement that is
    manufactured from clinker that contains more than 0.5% by weight of
    ferric oxide. ([ciment gris]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[kiln]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[kiln]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a thermally insulated chamber
    into which blended feedstock is introduced for pyroprocessing in
    order to produce clinker. ([four]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[long dry kiln]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[long dry kiln]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a kiln that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is not equipped with a system for preheating dry
      feedstock; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is equipped with a system for preheating dry
      feedstock with at most two stages of preheating before the
      feedstock is introduced into the kiln. ([four long à voie
      sèche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[precalciner kiln]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[precalciner kiln]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a kiln that is
    equipped with a system for precalcining dry feedstock using, before
    the feedstock is introduced into the kiln, a secondary burner that
    has a tertiary supply of combustion air. ([four à
    précalcinateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[preheater kiln]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[preheater kiln]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a kiln that is equipped
    with a system for preheating dry feedstock with at least three
    stages of preheating before the feedstock is introduced into the
    kiln. ([four à préchauffeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[wet kiln]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[wet kiln]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a kiln into which feedstock
    is introduced as a fine slurry with a water content of more than 20%
    by weight. ([four à voie humide]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- grey cement

**[[102]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies in
respect of cement manufacturing facilities that produce clinker for use
in the manufacture of grey cement.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation --- over two consecutive years

- **[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} As
  of January 1, 2020, a cement manufacturing facility must not --- over
  any two consecutive years --- emit NO~x~ or SO~2~ in a quantity that
  exceeds the emission limit for each of those years for that substance,
  as determined in accordance with sections 104 and 105, respectively.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation --- yearly

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a cement manufacturing facility does not comply
  with that emission limit for either NO~x~ or SO~2~ over a period of
  two consecutive years, it must not --- during each year after that
  period --- emit either NO~x~ or SO~2~ in a quantity that exceeds its
  emission limit for that year, as determined in accordance with
  sections 104 and 105.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Emission limit --- NO~x~

- **[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  emission limit for a year for the emission of NO~x~ from a cement
  manufacturing facility is determined by the formula

  ::: {#829555 .Subsection}
  Σ~i~(EI~NOxi~ × P~i~)/Σ~i~P~i~

  where

  [EI~NOxi~]{.dfn}
  :   is the maximum emission intensity for the year for the emission of
      NO~x~ from the ith kiln stack in the cement manufacturing facility
      --- namely, the maximum quantity of NO~x~ emitted per tonne of
      clinker produced at the ith kiln in the cement manufacturing
      facility in the year --- which is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for preheater kilns and precalciner kilns, 2.25
        kg/t, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for wet kilns and long dry kilns, as elected in
        accordance with subsection (2),

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 2.55 kg/t, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} determined by the formula

          ::: {#829562 .NestedFormula}
          EI~2006~ -- (0.3 × EI~2006~)

          where

          [EI~2006~]{.dfn}
          :   is the quantity of NO~x~, expressed in kg, that was
              emitted at the cement manufacturing facility in 2006 per
              tonne of clinker produced, as reported in respect of the
              cement manufacturing facility to the Minister in
              accordance with the Notice with respect to reporting of
              information on air pollutants, greenhouse gases and other
              substances for the 2006 calendar year, published in the
              [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/), Part I,
              Volume 141, No. 49, on December 8, 2007;
          :::

  [i]{.dfn}
  :   is ith kiln in the cement manufacturing facility, where i goes
      from 1 to n and where n is the number of kilns in the cement
      manufacturing facility; and

  [P~i~]{.dfn}
  :   is the quantity of clinker, expressed in t, that is produced by
      the ith kiln in the cement manufacturing facility in the year.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election --- 2020

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A responsible person for the cement manufacturing
  facility who provides the Minister with a compliance report required
  by section 108 in respect of the year 2020 must, in that compliance
  report, make the election referred to in paragraph (b) of the
  description of EI~NOxi~ in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election --- subsequent years

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The maximum emission intensity for the emission of
  NO~x~ from the ith kiln in the cement manufacturing facility for the
  year 2020 that is elected by the responsible person also applies for
  each subsequent year.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Emission limit --- SO~2~

**[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105 .sectionLabel}** The emission limit for
a year for the emission of SO~2~ from a cement manufacturing facility is
determined by the formula

::: {#829573 .Section}
Σ~i~(EI~SO2i~ × P~i~)/Σ~i~P~i~

where

[EI~SO2i~]{.dfn}
:   is the maximum emission intensity for the year for the emission of
    SO~2~ from the ith kiln stack in the cement manufacturing facility
    --- namely, the maximum quantity of SO~2~ emitted per tonne of
    clinker produced at the ith kiln in the cement manufacturing
    facility in the year --- which is 3.0 kg/t;

[i]{.dfn}
:   is the ith kiln in the cement manufacturing facility, where i goes
    from 1 to n and where n is the number of kilns in the cement
    manufacturing facility; and

[P~i~]{.dfn}
:   is the quantity of clinker, expressed in t, that is produced by the
    ith kiln in the cement manufacturing facility in the year.
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantity of NO~x~ and SO~2~ --- CEMS

**[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106 .sectionLabel}** As of January 1, 2018,
the quantity, expressed in kg, of NO~x~ and SO~2~ emitted, during a
given year, from each kiln stack in a cement manufacturing facility must
be determined by means of a CEMS.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantity of clinker

- **[[107]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purpose of determining the value for P~i~ in sections 104 and 105,
  the quantity of clinker that is produced at each kiln in the cement
  manufacturing facility in the year must be determined by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} weighing that quantity directly using the measuring
    devices that were used for inventory purposes;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} using a method of calculation that is based on the
    measuring devices that were used for inventory purposes; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} applying a feedstock-to-clinker conversion factor,
    specific to the kiln, to a direct measurement of the quantity of
    feedstock introduced into the kiln during that year, which
    accurately determines the quantity of clinker that is produced from
    a given quantity of feedstock introduced.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accuracy of feedstock-to-clinker
  conversion factor

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The accuracy of the feedstock-to-clinker conversion
  factor must be verified

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least once per year, but at least four months
    after a previous verification; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as soon as feasible after a major change in the
    clinker production processes that could affect the accuracy of the
    factor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record-making

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A record of that verification must be made that
  demonstrates the accuracy of the feedstock-to-clinker conversion
  factor.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance report

**[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108 .sectionLabel}** As of January 1, 2019,
on or before June 1 of every year a compliance report that contains the
information for the cement manufacturing facility that is set out in
Schedule 11 in respect of the preceding year must be provided to the
Minister.

## [PART 4]{.HLabel1}[General]{.HTitleText1} {#h-829594 .Part}

### [Continuous Emission Monitoring Systems]{.HTitleText2} {#h-829595 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}CEMS Reference Method --- compliance

**[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109 .sectionLabel}** If a CEMS is used to
determine a NO~x~ emission intensity under Part 1 or a quantity of
emissions under section 106, the CEMS Reference Method must be complied
with, notably

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the design specifications and any related
  requirements that are set out in

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Section 3.0 of the EC CEMS Code entitled "Design
    Specifications and Test Procedures", or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} Section 2.0 of the Alberta CEMS Code entitled
    "Design Specifications";

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the installation specifications and any related
  requirements that are set out in

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Section 4.0 of the EC CEMS Code entitled
    "Installation Specifications", or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} Section 3.0 of the Alberta CEMS Code entitled
    "Installation Specifications";

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the performance specifications and any related
  requirements, particularly in respect of testing, that are set out in

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Section 5.0 of the EC CEMS Code entitled
    "Certification Performance Specifications and Test Procedures",
    including certification of the CEMS and the conduct of relative
    accuracy tests and bias tests of the relative accuracy test audit
    (RATA), or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} Section 4.0 of the Alberta CEMS Code entitled
    "Performance Specifications and Test Procedures", including
    certification of the CEMS and the conduct of relative accuracy tests
    and bias tests of the relative accuracy test audit (RATA); and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the requirements concerning the development and
  implementation of manuals or plans for quality assurance and quality
  control that are set out in

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Section 6.0 of the EC CEMS Code entitled "Quality
    Assurance and Quality Control", or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} Section 5.0 of the Alberta CEMS Code entitled
    "Quality Assurance and Quality Control".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual audit

- **[[110]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-110 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  each year during which a responsible person uses a CEMS to determine a
  NO~x~ emission intensity under Part 1 or a quantity of emissions under
  section 106, the responsible person must ensure that an auditor
  evaluates whether, in the auditor's opinion, the responsible person
  has complied with the CEMS Reference Method and, in particular,
  whether

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the CEMS has met the specifications set out in the
    CEMS Reference Method;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the results of each relative accuracy test and each
    bias test for each of the relative accuracy test audits (RATA) for
    the 12 months preceding the auditor's audit have not exceeded the
    applicable limit referred to in the CEMS Reference Method; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the responsible person has established a Quality
    Assurance/Quality Control manual that complies with Section 6.0 of
    the EC CEMS Code or a Quality Assurance Plan that complies with
    Section 5.1 of the Alberta CEMS Code and, if so, whether

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the responsible person has implemented it,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} its implementation ensures that the data
      generated by the CEMS is complete, accurate and precise, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} it has been updated in accordance with the CEMS
      Reference Method.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Auditor's report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The responsible person must, within 30 days after the
  day on which the auditor completes their audit, obtain a report,
  signed by the auditor, that contains the information set out in
  Schedule 12.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for auditor's report

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The responsible person must, on the Minister's
  request, provide the Minister with a copy of an auditor's report
  within 15 days after the request.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of auditor's report to
  Minister

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A responsible person who uses a CEMS to determine a
  NO~x~ emission intensity under Part 1 must, for each year during which
  they use a CEMS, provide the Minister with a copy of the auditor's
  report for that year by June 1 of the following year, if the opinion
  stated by the auditor in the report is that the responsible person has
  not complied with any aspect of the CEMS Reference Method.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Auditor

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of this section, an auditor is a
  person who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is independent of the responsible person who uses a
    CEMS; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has demonstrated knowledge of and experience in

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the certification, operation and relative
      accuracy test audit (RATA) of Continuous Emission Monitoring
      Systems,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} quality assurance and quality control procedures
      in relation to those systems, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the conducting of audits in relation to those
      systems.

### [Measuring Devices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-829632 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Installation, operation, maintenance and
calibration

**[[111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111 .sectionLabel}** Unless otherwise
provided by these Regulations, any measuring device that is used to
determine a quantity for the purpose of these Regulations must be
installed, operated, maintained and calibrated in accordance with the
manufacturer's specifications or any applicable generally recognized
national or international industry standard.

### [Alternative Rules]{.HTitleText2} {#h-829635 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[112]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may --- on written application by a responsible person for a
  boiler, heater, engine or cement manufacturing facility --- approve an
  alternative rule in respect of the boiler, heater or engine or a kiln
  located in the cement manufacturing facility to replace a rule set out
  in a document that is incorporated by reference into these Regulations
  with respect to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any requirement for sampling, analyses, tests,
    measurements or monitoring; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any condition, test procedure or laboratory
    practice that is relevant to those requirements.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception for rule to be replaced

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The rule that is to be replaced must not be a rule
  that is contained in EPA Method 7E or EPA Method 6C in relation to the
  conduct of relative accuracy tests and bias tests of the relative
  accuracy test audit (RATA) referred to in subparagraph 109(c)(i) or
  (ii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative rule

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The proposed alternative rule must be a rule that the
  responsible person may comply with in order to comply with a
  provincial law in respect of the boiler, heater, engine or kiln and be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a modification of a rule set out in a document that
    is incorporated by reference in these Regulations; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a rule, other than a rule referred to in paragraph
    (a), that consists of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the whole or a part of a standard or method
      published by

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a government of any state or of a subdivision
        of any state, or any institution of such a state or subdivision,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} an international organization of states or an
        international organization that is established by the
        governments of states, or any institution of one of those
        international organizations, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} an organization that develops standards or
        methods based on consensus and that is internationally
        recognized as being competent to establish that standard or
        method; or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a modification of a rule described in
      subparagraph (i).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information for application

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The application must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the following information respecting the
    responsible person who is the applicant:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} their name, civic address and email address,
      along with an indication as to whether they are an owner or
      operator, or both, of the boiler, heater, engine or cement
      manufacturing facility,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses, if
      any, telephone number and email address of their authorized
      official, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses, if
      any, telephone number and email address of a contact person, if
      different from the authorized official;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for each responsible person for the boiler, heater,
    engine or cement manufacturing facility other than the applicant,
    their name, civic address and email address, along with an
    indication as to whether they are an owner or operator of it, or
    both;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an indication of the rule that is to be replaced,
    along with an indication of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the provisions of the document incorporated by
      reference into these Regulations that set out the rule that is to
      be replaced, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the provisions of these Regulations that refer
      to that document and that invoke that rule;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the text of the proposed alternative rule, along
    with

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the source of that proposed alternative rule,
      namely,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} an indication of the provisions of provincial
        law that set out that proposed alternative rule,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a copy of a permit issued under provincial law
        along with an indication of the provisions of the permit that
        set out that proposed alternative rule, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the sources referred to in both clauses (A) and
        (B), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an indication of the provisions of the standard
      or method referred to in subparagraph (3)(b)(i) that constitutes
      the proposed alternative rule or that were modified to constitute
      the proposed alternative rule, along with a copy of that standard
      or method;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a demonstration that the proposed alternative rule
    --- read in the entire context of the rule that is to be replaced,
    including the context of the document within which that replaced
    rule is found, and read harmoniously within the scheme of these
    Regulations --- is at least as rigorous and effective as the rule
    that is to be replaced;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} information that identifies the boiler, heater,
    engine or kiln, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the boiler or heater,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether it is a boiler or a
        heater,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the name of its manufacturer, along with its
        serial number, make and model,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} its rated capacity, and

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the facility where it is located,
        its identifier, if any, within that facility and the facility's

        - [(I)]{.lawlabel} civic address,

        - [(II)]{.lawlabel} National Pollutant Release Inventory
          identification number assigned by the Minister for the purpose
          of section 48 of the Act, and

        - [(III)]{.lawlabel} provincial identification number,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for the engine,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} its serial number or, if the serial number is
        not known or cannot be obtained, its unique alphanumeric
        identifier,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} its make and model, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the facility where it is located,
        its identifier, if any, within that facility and the facility's

        - [(I)]{.lawlabel} civic address or, if there is no civic
          address, its latitude and longitude,

        - [(II)]{.lawlabel} National Pollutant Release Inventory
          identification number assigned by the Minister for the purpose
          of section 48 of the Act, and

        - [(III)]{.lawlabel} provincial identification number,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for the kiln ,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the name of the cement manufacturing facility
        where it is located,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the cement manufacturing
        facility or, if there is no civic address, its latitude and
        longitude,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the cement manufacturing facility's National
        Pollutant Release Inventory identification number assigned by
        the Minister for the purpose of section 48 of the Act,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} information that identifies the kiln, and

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the kiln's type, that is, whether it is

        - [(I)]{.lawlabel} a precalciner kiln,

        - [(II)]{.lawlabel} a preheater kiln,

        - [(III)]{.lawlabel} a wet kiln, or

        - [(IV)]{.lawlabel} a long dry kiln; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any other information that is necessary to consider
    in order to grant the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Paragraph (4)(e) --- concentration of
  NO~x~

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a proposed alternative rule described in paragraph
  (3)(b) concerns the determination of the concentration of NO~x~ in a
  gas, other than by means of a CEMS, the demonstration referred to in
  paragraph (4)(e) must compare its rigour and effectiveness to the
  rigour and effectiveness of making that determination based on EPA
  Method 7E or ASTM D6522-11.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

- **[[113]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must grant the application and approve the proposed
  alternative rule --- with any modification, or subject to any
  conditions, that the Minister considers desirable --- if the Minister
  is of the opinion that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the rule that is to be replaced is described by
    paragraph 112(1)(a) or (b);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the proposed alternative rule is described by
    paragraph 112(3)(a) or (b); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the proposed alternative rule --- read in the
    entire context of the rule that is to be replaced, including the
    context of the document within which that replaced rule is found,
    and read harmoniously within the scheme of these Regulations --- is
    at least as rigorous and effective as the rule that is to be
    replaced.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of approved alternative rule

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Without delay after approving an alternative rule,
  the Minister must provide the applicant --- and every other
  responsible person for the boiler, heater or engine, or the cement
  manufacturing facility in which the kiln is located, set out in the
  application --- with the alternative rule, as approved.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of rule to another
  responsible person

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A responsible person referred to in subsection (2)
  must --- on another person becoming a responsible person for it ---
  provide that other person with the alternative rule, as approved.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of approved alternative rule

- **[[114]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-114
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 115, an
  alternative rule, as approved by the Minister, replaces the rule in
  question that is set out in a document that is incorporated by
  reference into these Regulations and applies in respect of the boiler,
  heater, engine or kiln located in the cement manufacturing facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation of incorporated document

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the document is to be read
  with reference to the alternative rule, as approved, and not the rule
  that the alternative rule replaces.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to apply replaced rule

**[[115]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-115 .sectionLabel}** A responsible person
for the boiler, heater or engine, or the cement manufacturing facility
in which the kiln is located, may in writing notify the Minister and
every other responsible person for it that the alternative rule, as
approved, no longer applies --- as of the date indicated in the notice
--- in respect of that boiler, heater, engine or kiln located in the
cement manufacturing facility.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal --- false, misleading or
incomplete information

- **[[116]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-116 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must refuse the application if the Minister has reasonable
  grounds to believe that the responsible person has, with their
  application, provided false, misleading or incomplete information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal --- informing applicant

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Without delay after refusing the application, the
  Minister must inform the applicant of the refusal, along with the
  reasons for it.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation by Minister

- **[[117]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-117 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must revoke the alternative rule as approved by the Minister
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} new information, with supporting documents,
    demonstrates that the alternative rule, as approved --- read in the
    entire context of the rule that is to be replaced, including the
    context of the document within which that replaced rule is found,
    and read harmoniously within the scheme of these Regulations --- is
    not at least as rigorous and effective as the rule it replaces; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that
    the responsible person had, with their application, provided false
    or misleading information to the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation --- informing responsible
  persons

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Without delay after the revocation, the Minister must
  notify each responsible person for the boiler, heater or engine, or
  the cement manufacturing facility in which the kiln is located, of the
  revocation, along with the reasons for it and an indication of the
  date of the revocation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation under law

**[[118]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-118 .sectionLabel}** The alternative rule as
approved by the Minister is revoked as of the day on which the
responsible person no longer is able to comply with provincial law by
complying with the alternative rule in respect of the boiler, heater,
engine or kiln located in the cement manufacturing facility. Without
delay after that day, the responsible person must notify the Minister in
writing of that revocation, including an indication of the date of that
day.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rule after notification or revocation

**[[119]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-119 .sectionLabel}** The rule set out in a
document that is incorporated by reference into these Regulations and
referred to in subsection 112(1) that was replaced by an alternative
rule, as approved by the Minister, applies in respect of the boiler,
heater, engine or kiln located in the cement manufacturing facility on

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} receipt by the Minister of the notice referred to in
  section 115;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of the revocation that is indicated in the
  notice described in subsection 117(2); or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date of the revocation referred to in section
  118.

### [Reporting, Providing, Recording and Retention of Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-829734 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic provision

- **[[120]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  report, notice or information that is required to be provided, or an
  application that is made, to the Minister under these Regulations must
  be provided electronically in the form and format specified by the
  Minister and must bear the signature or electronic signature of an
  authorized official of the responsible person who provides it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision on paper

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister has not specified an electronic form
  and format or if it is impractical to provide the report, notice,
  information or application in accordance with subsection (1) because
  of circumstances beyond the control of the responsible person, the
  report , notice, information or application must be provided on paper,
  signed by an authorized official of the responsible person, in the
  form and format specified by the Minister. However, if no form and
  format have been so specified, it may be in any form and format.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record-making

- **[[121]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-121 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  record in respect of a boiler, heater, engine or cement manufacturing
  facility must, to the extent that it is not otherwise required under
  these Regulations, be made

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} of every document and all information that supports
    the validity of any information provided to the Minister under these
    Regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} of every measurement and calculation, along with
    supporting documents, that are used to determine a value of an
    element of a formula set out in these Regulations, as well as any
    information, including the methodology, that is used to determine
    one of those values;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if a CEMS is used under these Regulations,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} of every document or information referred to in
      the CEMS Reference Method that

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} relates to a demonstration or test made under
        these Regulations, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} is required to be created or obtained under the
        CEMS Reference Method, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} of every measurement of a concentration and of
      flow that is used for every calculation necessary to determine an
      emission intensity, along with supporting documents;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} of documents that demonstrate that the
    installation, operation, maintenance and calibration of measuring
    devices was done in accordance with these Regulations; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} of any other information that is relevant to
    demonstrating compliance with these Regulations in respect of the
    boiler, heater, engine or cement manufacturing facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records --- deadline

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Records required to be made under these Regulations
  must be made within 30 days after the day on which the information to
  be recorded becomes available.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Five or ten years' retention

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A record or application that is made --- or a copy of
  a report, notice or information that is provided --- under these
  Regulations must be kept, along any supporting documents, after it is
  made or provided for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least five years if it relates to a boiler,
    heater, or an engine; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least 10 years if it relates to a cement
    manufacturing facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No retention

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (3), any information that has been
  provided to the Minister by a responsible person for inclusion in the
  engine registry or in an online electronic reporting site that is
  established by the Minister does not need to be kept if the Minister
  has provided the responsible person with an acknowledgment of its
  receipt.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record location --- Parts 1 and 3

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The record or copy referred to in subsection (3) ---
  other than one that relates to an engine --- must be kept at the
  facility where the boiler or heater is located or at the cement
  manufacturing facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record location --- Part 2

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The record or the copy referred to in subsection (3)
  that relates to an engine must be kept at

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any place in Canada other than the facility where
    the engine is located, if that place is, when the engine is
    registered, indicated in the engine registry; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the facility where the engine is located, in any
    other case.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of records

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} On the Minister's request, the record or copy
  referred to in subsections (5) and (6) must be provided to the
  Minister without delay.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corrections

**[[122]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122 .sectionLabel}** A responsible person
must, without delay, inform the Minister of any errors contained in
information that was provided to Minister under these Regulations and
provide the Minister with the corrected information.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of testing

- **[[123]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  responsible person for a boiler or heater who intends, by means of a
  stack test, to determine its NO~x~ emission intensity for the purpose
  of sections 33 to 38 or a responsible person for an engine who intends
  to conduct a performance test on it under clause 66(2)(a)(i)(B) or
  section 77 or 78 or an emissions check on it under section 79 must, on
  the Minister's request, provide the Minister with the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the place where the test or
    check is to be conducted or the determination or redetermination
    made or, if there is no civic address, its latitude and longitude;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date or dates on which it is to occur; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number and email address of a
    contact person who can provide the Minister with any updated
    information in relation to paragraphs (a) and (b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When information provided

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The information must be provided

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least 30 days before the test or check is
    conducted or the determination or redetermination is made, if that
    test, check, determination or redetermination is intended to be
    conducted or made more than 30 days after the receipt of the
    request; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} without delay, in any other case.

## [Coming into Force]{.HTitleText1} {#h-829805 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration

- **[[148]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to (5),
  these Regulations come into force on the day on which they are
  registered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}January 1, 2021

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 135 comes into force on January 1, 2021.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}January 1, 2023

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The following provisions come into force on January
  1, 2023:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 125(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} section 128 ;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subsection 129(2);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subsection 130(1);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} subsection 131(1); and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} subsection 145(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}January 1, 2026

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The following provisions come into force on January
  1, 2026:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 126(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsection 129(1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subsection 130(2);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} sections 132 to 134;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} sections 136 to 144;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} subsection 145(1); and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} sections 146 and 147.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}January 1, 2036

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The following provisions come into force on January
  1, 2036:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} section 124;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsection 125(2);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subsection 126(2);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} section 127;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} subsection 129(3);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} subsection 131(2); and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} subsection 145(3).

## [SCHEDULE 1]{.scheduleLabel}[(Subsection 2(3))]{.OriginatingRef}[EC CEMS Code Modifications]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-829838 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** The EC CEMS Code is to be read as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} without reference to Section 1.0 entitled
    "Introduction", including any cross-references to any provision in
    Section 1.0;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} without reference to "appropriate regulatory
    authority" in Table 1 entitled "Design Specifications for Continuous
    Emission Monitoring Systems" ;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} without reference to "appropriate regulatory
    authority" in the following Sections:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 3.4,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 3.4.2,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} 3.4.3,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} 5.3.1, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} 6.3.2.7;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} with "Other moisture monitoring systems may be
    proposed for use with Equation B-3 in Appendix B, if the proponent
    demonstrates that the system meets the required specifications." in
    the note to the "Stack gas and moisture monitor" component of Table
    3 entitled "Certification of Performance Specifications" read as
    "Other moisture monitoring systems may be used with Equation B-3 in
    Appendix B, if the system meets the required specifications.";

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} with "an independent reference method, which may be
    either a manual or automated procedure, as specified by the
    appropriate regulatory authority." in Section 5.3.4 read as "EPA
    Method 7E or EPA Method 6C, as the case may be, along with EPA
    Method 3A and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 1 or EC Method A,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 2 or EC Method B, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 4 or EC Method D.";

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} with "Either integrating manual or automated
    methods specified by the appropriate regulatory authority may be
    used as the reference methods for this test." in Section 5.3.4.3
    read as "EPA Method 7E or EPA Method 6C, as the case may be, are to
    be used as the reference method for this test, along with EPA Method
    3A and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 1 or EC Method A,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 2 or EC Method B, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 4 or EC Method D.";

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} without reference to "In collaboration with the
    regulatory agency" in Section 6.0 entitled "Quality Assurance and
    Quality Control" ;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} without reference to "and the appropriate agency"
    in Section 6.5.2;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} with the Glossary read without reference to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the definition [[appropriate regulatory
      authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the definition
      [[backfilling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the definition [[units of the
      standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm};

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} with the definition [[reference
    method]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in the Glossary read as "means EPA
    Method 7E or EPA Method 6C, as the case may be, along with EPA
    Method 3A and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 1 or EC Method A,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 2 or EC Method B, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 4 or EC Method D.";

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} without reference to ", but any factors so
    developed will require approval by the appropriate regulatory agency
    before being applied" in Section A.1 of Appendix A;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} with the values of K~x~ for SO~2~ and NO~x~ in
    Equations A-1 to A-7 referred to in Section A.2 of Appendix A read
    as

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the value of SO~2~, "2.618 × 10^--6^
      kg/Sm^3^/ppm", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for the value of NO~x~, "1.880 × 10^--6^
      kg/Sm^3^/ppm";

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} with Section B.2.1 of Appendix B read without
    reference to "appropriate regulatory agency"; and

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} without reference to Section B.4 entitled "Method
    C: Energy Balance Method" in Appendix B.

## [SCHEDULE 2]{.scheduleLabel}[(Subsection 2(4))]{.OriginatingRef}[Alberta CEMS Code Modifications]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-829875 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** The Alberta CEMS Code is to be read as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with any table that sets out specifications read as
    establishing an obligation on the responsible person to respect
    those specifications;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} without reference to Section 1.0;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} without reference to "Unless otherwise authorized
    by the Director," in Section 2.0;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} without reference to Section 2.2;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} without reference in Section 2.5.1 to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "as specified in the facility approval",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "in a format acceptable to the Director", and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} ", if required by an approval";

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} without reference to "or those hours during which
    effluent is being discharged from an effluent source as described in
    an approval (for noncombustion-related sources)" in Section 2.5.3;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} without reference in Section 2.5.4 to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "Upon the authorization of the Director,",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "Reference Method test data or data obtained
      from a monitor previously certified for the application may also
      be used for substituting data.",

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} ", and must be authorized in writing by the
      Director prior to implementation", and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} ", unless specified otherwise by the Director";

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} with "Method 1 of the Alberta Stack Sampling Code
    as amended from time to time" in Section 3.1 read as "EPA Method 1
    or EC Method A";

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} with "Method 1 of the Alberta Stack Sampling Code"
    in Section 3.1.3 read as "EPA Method 1 or EC Method A" ;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} with the portion of Section 3.2 before Section
    3.2.1 read without reference to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "or at a location authorized by the Director",
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "or an alternative method as authorized by the
      Director, may be used";

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} in Section 3.2.1, with

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "Method 1 of the Alberta Stack Sampling Code"
      read as "EPA Method 1 or EC Method A", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "Method 2 of the Alberta Stack Sampling Code"
      read as "EPA Method 2 or EC Method B";

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} with "source owner or operator" in the portion of
    Section 4.1 before Section 4.1.1 read as "responsible person";

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} in Section 4.1.1,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} without reference to "Subject to Section 1.5.1,"

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} with "owner or operator of the facility" read as
      "responsible person", and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} without reference to "of a new CEMS, upon
      recertification, or as specified otherwise by the Director. The
      satisfactory demonstration by the approval holder of meeting all
      of these performance specifications, along with notice of such to
      the Director, shall constitute certification of the CEMS.";

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} without reference in Section 4.2.1 to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} ", subject to the provisions of an applicable
      approval,", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "These specifications are not meant to limit the
      types of technologies that can be used or prevent the use of
      equivalent methods. Both technologies and methods can be varied
      upon authorization of the Director.";

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} without reference in Section 4.2.2 to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} ", subject to the provisions of an applicable
      approval,", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "These specifications are not meant to limit the
      types of technologies that can be used or prevent the use of
      equivalent methods. Both technologies and methods can be varied
      upon the written authorization of the Director.";

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} without reference in Section 4.2.3 to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} ", subject to the provisions of an applicable
      approval,", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "These specifications are not meant to limit the
      use of alternative technology and may be varied upon the written
      authorization of the Director to accommodate the use of
      alternative technology.";

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} without reference to Section 4.2.4;

  - [(r)]{.lawlabel} without reference in Section 4.2.5 to "These
    specifications are not meant to limit the types of technologies to
    be used or prevent the use of equivalent methods (such as the use of
    F-factors). Both technologies and methods can be varied upon written
    authorization of the Director.";

  - [(s)]{.lawlabel} in Section 4.2.6,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} with "approval holder" read as "responsible
      person",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} without reference to ", as specified in an EPEA
      approval", and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} without reference to "These specifications are
      not meant to limit the types of technologies to be used or prevent
      the use of equivalent methods. Both technologies and methods can
      be varied upon the written authorization of the Director.";

  - [(t)]{.lawlabel} without reference to Section 4.2.7;

  - [(u)]{.lawlabel} without reference in Section 4.3 to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "The Director must be advised in writing (or
      facsimile) of the intent to test (complete with tentative test
      schedule\[s\]) at least two weeks before the performance testing
      is to occur. This notice enables the Director or his/her designate
      to have the opportunity to observe any or all testing.",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "The owner or operator of the facility shall
      retain on file at the facility, and make available for inspection
      or audit, the performance test results on which the certification
      was based.",

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} "Recertification is required following any
      major change in the CEMS (e.g., addition of components or
      replacement of components with different makes/models, change in
      gas cells, path length, probe or system optics, relocation) that
      could impair the system from meeting the applicable performance
      specifications for that system. Recertification should be
      conducted at the earliest possible opportunity or as agreed to in
      writing by the Director.", and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} "or recertification";

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} without reference in Section 4.5.1 to "The system
    must output emission rates of the pollutants in units as specified
    in the facility approval.";

  - [(w)]{.lawlabel} without reference in Section 4.5.3 to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "**(a) General** - For those systems that are not
      designed (and authorized as such by the Director) for the dynamic
      use of calibration gases, alternative protocols (as authorized by
      the Director) may be used in place of the following. These
      alternative procedures shall be included and detailed in the
      facility QAP.", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "(to the extent possible)";

  - [(x)]{.lawlabel} without reference in Section 4.5.4 to "**(a)
    General** - For those systems that are not designed (and authorized
    as such by the Director) for the dynamic use of calibration gases,
    alternative protocols (as authorized by the Director) may be used in
    place of the following. These alternative procedures shall be
    included and detailed in the facility QAP." ;

  - [(y)]{.lawlabel} with "in accordance with the Alberta Stack Sampling
    Code, and in such a manner that they will yield results
    representative of the pollutant concentration, emission rate,
    moisture content, temperature and effluent flow rate from the unit
    and can be correlated with the CEMS measurements." in Section
    4.5.8(d) read as "in such a manner that they will yield results
    representative of the pollutant concentration, emission rate,
    moisture content, temperature and effluent flow rate from the unit
    and can be correlated with the CEMS measurements, and in accordance
    with EPA Method 7E or EPA Method 6C, as the case may be, along with
    EPA Method 3A and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 1 or EC Method A,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 2 or EC Method B, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 4 or EC Method D.";

  - [(z)]{.lawlabel} with "approval holder" read as "responsible person"
    in Section 5.0;

  - [(z.1)]{.lawlabel} without reference to "(where authorized)" in
    Table 14 to Section 5.1.2;

  - [(z.2)]{.lawlabel} in Section 5.1.3,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in paragraph (a),

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} with "approval holder" read as "responsible
        person",

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} with "sample system flow rates are
        appropriate). The use of system components with integral fault
        detection diagnostics is highly desirable" read as "the use of
        sample system flow rates and the use of system components with
        integral fault detection diagnostics)", and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} without reference to "The minimum frequency may
        be reduced (upon the written authorization of the Director)
        provided the operator can demonstrate (using historical data)
        that a lower verification frequency will not affect system
        performance at the 95% confidence level.",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in paragraph (b), without reference to "For
      parameters for which cylinder gases are not available at
      reasonable cost, are unstable, or are unavailable, alternative
      calibration techniques are acceptable, if the Director has given
      prior written authorization.", and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in paragraph (e), without reference to "The
      approval holder must retain the results of all performance
      evaluations including raw test data as well as all maintenance
      logs, corrective action logs and the QAP (including sample
      calculations) for a period of at least 3 years for inspection by
      Alberta Environmental Protection.";

  - [(z.3)]{.lawlabel} in Section 5.2.1,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} with "approval holder" read as "responsible
      person",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} with "upon the Director being satisfied that the
      QAP demonstrates compliance" read as "if the responsible person
      demonstrates compliance", and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} without reference to "The data obtained during
      a Relative Accuracy Test may also be used toward fulfilling
      associated stack survey requirements as provided for in an
      approval issued under EPEA.";

  - [(z.4)]{.lawlabel} without reference in Section 5.2.3 to "For those
    systems that are not designed for the dynamic use of calibration
    gases, alternative protocols (as authorized by the Director) may be
    used in place of the cylinder gas audit. These alternative
    procedures shall be included and detailed in the facility QAP." ;

  - [(z.5)]{.lawlabel} in Section 5.2.4,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} with "facility operator" read as "responsible
      person",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} with " the facility should be operated at a rate
      of at least 90 % of "normal" production. Normal production is
      defined as the average production or throughput for the facility
      over the previous month. Any exceptions to this would need to be
      authorized in writing by the Director." read as " the boiler,
      heater, engine or cement manufacturing facility must be operated
      at a rate of at least 90% of "normal" production. Normal
      production is defined as its average production over the previous
      month.", and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} without reference to ", unless otherwise
      authorized by the Director";

  - [(z.6)]{.lawlabel} without reference to Section 6.0; and

  - [(z.7)]{.lawlabel} in the appendix entitled "APPENDIX A --
    DEFINITIONS",

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} without reference to the definitions

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} [[Alberta Stack Sampling
        Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} [[Director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} [[Emission standard
        level]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} with the definition [[Reference
      Method]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} read as " means EPA Method 7E or EPA
      Method 6C, as the case may be, along with EPA Method 3A and

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 1 or EC Method A,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 2 or EC Method B, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} EPA Method 4 or EC Method D.".

## [SCHEDULE 5]{.scheduleLabel}[(Sections 4 and 41 and paragraph 43(1)(g))]{.OriginatingRef}[Classification Report (Boilers and Heaters) --- Information Required]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-829995 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** The following information respecting the responsible person:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether they are an owner or
    operator, or both, of the boiler or heater and their name and civic
    address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses,
    telephone number, email address and fax number, if any, of their
    authorized official; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses,
    telephone number, email address and fax number, if any, of a contact
    person, if different from the authorized official.

- **2** The following information respecting the facility at which the
  boiler or heater is located:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the facility or, if there is
    no civic address, its latitude and longitude;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} its National Pollutant Release Inventory
    identification number assigned by the Minister for the purpose of
    section 48 of the Act and its provincial identification number, if
    any; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to which paragraph of subsection
    5(2) of these Regulations describes it.

- **3** The following information respecting the boiler or heater:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for each responsible person for the boiler or
    heater, other than a responsible person mentioned in paragraph 1(a),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} their name and civic address, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether they are an owner or
      operator, or both;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether it is a boiler or a
    heater;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of its manufacturer, along with its serial
    number, make and model;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} its rated capacity;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} its identifier, if any, within the facility where
    it is located;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} its commissioning date and recommissioning date, if
    any; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the date on which Part 1 first applied in respect
    of it;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} for a class 80 or a class 70 boiler or heater, the
    serial number of each of its burners; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for a class 80 or a class 70 boiler or heater, a
    document that indicates, with precision, where it is located in the
    facility.

- **4** An indication as to which of the following provisions of these
  Regulations is the one under which the boiler's or heater's
  classification NO~x~ emission intensity was determined:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subparagraph 34(1)(a)(i);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subparagraph 34(1)(a)(ii);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} clause 34(1)(b)(i)(A);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subclauses 34(1)(b)(i)(B)(I) and (II);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} subclauses 34(1)(b)(i)(B)(I), (II) and (III);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} subparagraph 34(1)(b)(ii);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} subparagraph 34(1)(b)(iii);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} subparagraph 34(1)(b)(iv);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subclause 34(1)(b)(v)(C)(I);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} subclause 34(1)(b)(v)(C)(II); or

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} subparagraph 34(1)(b)(vi).

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2016-151, s. 145\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2016-151, s. 145\]]{.Repealed}

- **5** The following information respecting a test on the boiler or
  heater referred to in paragraph 34(1)(a) of these Regulations
  conducted to determine its classification NO~x~ emission intensity:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a stack test,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the stack test was conducted,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} confirmation that each test run of the stack
      test was conducted while the boiler or heater met the conditions
      set out in paragraphs 27(2)(a) to (e) of these Regulations,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether the type of gaseous
      fossil fuel that was combusted during that stack test was natural
      gas or alternative gas,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the method set out in subsection 28(1) of these
      Regulations that was used for the stack test to measure the
      concentration of NO~x~ and, if an alternative rule as approved
      under subsection 113(1) of these Regulations to replace a rule set
      out in that method was used, an indication of that rule, as
      approved, and of rule it replaced, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the classification NO~x~ emission intensity of
      the boiler or heater, as determined by means of the stack test;
      and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a CEMS test,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for each averaging period in the reference
      period,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the day and hour when the averaging period
        began,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the number of hours in the averaging period,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} confirmation that at least 50% of the input
        energy in the boiler's or heater's combustion chamber resulted
        from the introduction of gaseous fossil fuel for each of those
        hours,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether the type of gaseous
        fossil fuel that was combusted was natural gas or alternative
        gas, and

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the greatest of the rolling hourly averages
        that were determined for the NO~x~ emission intensity,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether the EC CEMS Code or
      the Alberta CEMS Code was used and, if an alternative rule as
      approved under subsection 113(1) of these Regulations to replace a
      rule set out in that CEMS Reference Method was used, an indication
      of that rule, as approved, and of the rule it replaced, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the classification NO~x~ emission intensity of
      the boiler or heater, as determined by means of the CEMS test in
      accordance with subparagraph 34(1)(a)(ii) of these Regulations.

- **6** The following information respecting a stack test whose results
  are used to determine the boiler's or heater's classification NO~x~
  emission intensity under paragraph 34(1)(b) of these Regulations:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a stack test referred to in clause
    34(1)(b)(i)(A) --- or referred to in subclauses 34(1)(b)(i)(B)(I)
    and (II), if the stack test is on the boiler or heater in question
    referred to in clause 34(1)(b)(i)(B) --- of these Regulations,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the stack test was conducted,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} confirmation that each test run of the stack
      test was conducted while the boiler or heater met the conditions
      set out in paragraphs 27(2)(a) to (e) of these Regulations,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether the type of gaseous
      fossil fuel that was combusted during that stack test was natural
      gas or alternative gas,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the method set out in subsection 28(1) of these
      Regulations used for the stack test to measure the concentration
      of NO~x~ and, if an alternative rule as approved under subsection
      113(1) of these Regulations to replace a rule set out in that
      method was used, an indication of that rule, as approved, and of
      the rule it replaced, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the NO~x~ emission intensity of the boiler or
      heater, as determined by means of the stack test;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a stack test referred to in subclause
    34(1)(b)(i)(B)(III) or clause 34(1)(b)(iii)(B) of these Regulations,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the stack test was conducted,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name of the manufacturer --- along with the
      serial number, make and model and the identifier, if any, within
      the facility where it is located --- of the other boiler or heater
      on which the stack test was conducted, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the classification NO~x~ emission intensity of
      that other boiler or heater, as determined by means of the stack
      test; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for a stack test referred to in subclause
    34(1)(b)(v)(C)(I) of these Regulations,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the stack test was conducted,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name of the manufacturer --- along with the
      serial number, make and model and the identifier, if any, within
      the facility where it is located --- of each of the other boilers
      or heaters that share a common stack with the boiler or heater
      whose classification NO~x~ emission intensity is determined under
      that subclause,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} confirmation that each test run of the stack
      test was conducted while each of the boilers or heaters that share
      that common stack met the conditions set out in paragraphs
      27(2)(a) to (e) of these Regulations,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether the type of gaseous
      fossil fuel that was combusted during that stack test was natural
      gas or alternative gas, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the NO~x~ emission intensity at the common stack,
      as determined under subclause 34(1)(b)(v)(C)(I) of these
      Regulations.

- **7** The following information respecting a CEMS test whose results
  are used to determine the boiler's or heater's classification NO~x~
  emission intensity under paragraph 34(1)(b) of these Regulations:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a CEMS test referred to in clause
    34(1)(b)(iv)(B) of these Regulations,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of the manufacturer --- along with the
      serial number, make and model and the identifier, if any, within
      the facility where it is located --- of the other boiler or heater
      on which the CEMS test was conducted, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the classification NO~x~ emission intensity of
      that other boiler or heater, as determined by means of the CEMS
      test; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a CEMS test referred to in subclause
    34(1)(b)(v)(C)(II) of these Regulations,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of the manufacturer --- along with the
      serial number, make and model and, if any, the identifier within
      the facility where it is located --- of each of the other boilers
      or heaters that share a common stack with the boiler or heater
      whose classification NO~x~ emission intensity is determined under
      subparagraph 34(1)(b)(v) of these Regulations,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for each averaging period in the reference
      period,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the day and hour when the averaging period
        began,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the number of hours in the averaging period,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} confirmation that at least 50% of the input
        energy in the boiler's or heater's combustion chamber resulted
        from the introduction of gaseous fossil fuel for each of those
        hours,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether the type of gaseous
        fossil fuel that was combusted was natural gas or alternative
        gas, and

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the greatest of the rolling hourly averages
        that were determined for the NO~x~ emission intensity,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether the EC CEMS Code or
      the Alberta CEMS Code was used and, if an alternative rule as
      approved under subsection 113(1) of these Regulations to replace a
      rule set out in that CEMS Reference Method was used, an indication
      of that rule, as approved, and of the rule it replaced, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the NO~x~ emission intensity, being the greatest
      of the rolling hourly averages for the NO~x~ that were determined
      .

- **8** The following information respecting a boiler or heater whose
  classification NO~x~ emission intensity is determined under
  subparagraph 34(1)(b)(ii) of these Regulations:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} confirmation that there is no equipment installed
    on the boiler or heater that allows for a stack test or CEMS test to
    be conducted; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} documents that establish that the boiler or heater
    is designed to have a NO~x~ emission intensity of less than 40 g/GJ
    when the boiler or heater meets the conditions set out in paragraphs
    27(2)(a) to (e) of these Regulations and combusts the same type of
    gaseous fossil fuel --- natural gas or alternative gas --- that was
    combusted when that determination was made.
