## [SCHEDULE 6]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 4, subparagraph 14(1)(a)(i), section 40 and paragraphs 43(1)(a) to (f))]{.OriginatingRef}[Initial Report (Boilers and Heaters) --- Information Required]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-830082 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** The following information respecting the responsible person:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether they are an owner or
    operator, or both, of the boiler or heater and their name and civic
    address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses,
    telephone number, email address and fax number, if any, of their
    authorized official; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses,
    telephone number, email address and fax number, if any, of a contact
    person, if different from the authorized official.

- **2** The following information respecting the facility at which the
  boiler or heater is located:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the facility or, if there is
    no civic address, its latitude and longitude;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} its National Pollutant Release Inventory
    identification number assigned by the Minister for the purpose of
    section 48 of the Act and its provincial identification number, if
    any; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to which paragraph of subsection
    5(2) of these Regulations describes it.

- **3** The following information --- if it has changed since the most
  recent report that the responsible person has provided to the Minister
  --- respecting the boiler or heater:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for each responsible person for the boiler or
    heater, other than the responsible person referred to in paragraph
    1(a), if any,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} their name and civic address, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether they are an owner or
      operator, or both;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether it is a boiler or a
    heater;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of its manufacturer, along with its serial
    number, make and model;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} its rated capacity;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} its identifier, if any, within the facility where
    it is located;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} its commissioning date and recommissioning date, if
    any;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if its commissioning date is within three months
    after the day on which these Regulations are registered, an
    indication as to whether it is packaged;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} for a class 80 or a class 70 boiler or heater that
    has undergone a major modification, a description of the major
    modification, including the serial number of its burners if they
    have been changed;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for a modern boiler for which there has been a
    determination of its thermal efficiency made in accordance with
    section 18 of these Regulations, the result of each of those
    determinations and the date on which each was made;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} for a modern heater for which there has been a
    determination of the difference between the temperature of its
    preheated air and the ambient air made in accordance with section 24
    of these Regulations, the result of each of those determinations and
    the date on which each was made;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} for a boiler or heater for which the determination
    by means of one or more stack tests of its NO~x~ emission intensity
    for an initial test is referred to in paragraph 33(2)(a) or
    subparagraph 33(2)(b)(i) of these Regulations, for the stack test
    that determined the greatest NO~x~ emission intensity and for the
    stack test that determined the most recent NO~x~ emission intensity
    from among those stack tests conducted in the reference period or
    each applicable period within the reference period referred to in
    subsection 33(5) of these Regulations, respectively,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the NO~x~ emission intensity that was determined,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date on which that stack test was conducted,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} confirmation that each test run of the stack
      test was conducted while the boiler or heater met the conditions
      set out in paragraphs 27(2)(a) to (e) of these Regulations,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} for modern boilers or heaters, an indication as
      to whether the type of gaseous fossil fuel that was combusted
      during that stack test was natural gas or alternative gas, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the method set out in subsection 28(1) of these
      Regulations that was used for the stack test to measure the
      concentration of NO~x~ and, if an alternative rule as approved
      under subsection 113(1) of these Regulations to replace a rule set
      out in that method was used, an indication of that rule, as
      approved, and of the rule it replaced; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} for a boiler or heater for which the determination
    by means of a CEMS test of its NO~x~ emission intensity for an
    initial test is referred to in paragraph 33(2)(a) or subparapraph
    33(2)(b)(ii) of these Regulations,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for each averaging period in the reference
      period,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the day and hour when the averaging period
        began,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the number of hours in the averaging period,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} confirmation that at least 50% of the input
        energy in the boiler's or heater's combustion chamber resulted
        from the introduction of gaseous fossil fuel for each of those
        hours,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} for modern boilers or heaters, an indication as
        to whether type of the gaseous fossil fuel that was combusted
        was natural gas or alternative gas, and

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the NO~x~ emission intensity, being the
        greatest of the rolling hourly averages that were determined for
        the NO~x~ emission intensity,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether the EC CEMS Code or
      the Alberta CEMS Code was used and, if an alternative rule as
      approved under subsection 113(1) of these Regulations to replace a
      rule set out in that CEMS Reference Method was used, an indication
      of that rule, as approved, and of the rule it replaced, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for a determination referred to in paragraph
      33(2)(a), the name of the manufacturer, along with the serial
      number, make and model and the identifier, if any, within the
      facility where the other boiler or heater --- on which the CEMS
      test referred to in paragraph 26(2)(b) was conducted --- is
      located.
::::

## [SCHEDULE 7]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 4 and subsection 42(1))]{.OriginatingRef}[Compliance Report (Boilers and Heaters) --- Information Required]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-830121 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** The following information respecting the responsible person:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether they are an owner or
    operator, or both, of the boiler or heater and their name and civic
    address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses,
    telephone number, email address and fax number, if any, of their
    authorized official; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses,
    telephone number, email address and fax number, if any, of a contact
    person, if different from the authorized official.

- **2** The following information respecting the facility at which the
  boiler or heater is located:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the facility or, if there is
    no civic address, its latitude and longitude;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} its National Pollutant Release Inventory
    identification number assigned by the Minister for the purpose of
    section 48 of the Act and its provincial identification number, if
    any; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to which paragraph of subsection
    5(2) of these Regulations describes it.

- **3** The following information --- if it has changed since the most
  recent report that the responsible person has provided to the Minister
  --- respecting the boiler or heater:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for each responsible person for the boiler or
    heater, other than the responsible person referred to in paragraph
    1(a),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} their name and civic address, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether they are an owner or
      operator, or both;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of its manufacturer, along with its serial
    number, make and model;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} its rated capacity;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} its identifier, if any, within the facility where
    it is located;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} its commissioning date and recommissioning date, if
    any;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} for a modern boiler for which there has been a
    determination of its thermal efficiency made in accordance with
    section 18 of these Regulations, the result of each of those
    determinations and the date on which each was made;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} for a modern heater for which there has been a
    determination of the difference, if any, between the temperature of
    its preheated air and the ambient air made in accordance with
    section 24 of these Regulations, the result of each of those
    determinations and the date on which each was made;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} for a boiler or heater for which the determination
    by means of one or more stack tests of its NO~x~ emission intensity
    for a compliance test is referred to in subparagraph 38(2)(a)(i) or
    (c)(i) or paragraph 38(3)(a) of these Regulations, for the stack
    test that determined the greatest NO~x~ emission intensity and for
    the stack test that determined the most recent NO~x~ emission
    intensity, from among those stack tests conducted in the reference
    period or each applicable period referred to in paragraph 38(3)(a)
    or subsection 38(5) of these Regulations, respectively,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the NO~x~ emission intensity that was determined,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date on which that stack test was conducted,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} confirmation that each test run of that stack
      test was conducted while the boiler or heater met the conditions
      set out in paragraphs 27(2)(a) to (e) of these Regulations,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} for modern boilers or heaters, an indication as
      to whether the type of gaseous fossil fuel that was combusted
      during that stack test was natural gas or alternative gas, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the method set out in subsection 28(1) of these
      Regulations that was used for the stack test to measure the
      concentration of NO~x~ and, if an alternative rule as approved
      under subsection 113(1) of these Regulations to replace a rule set
      out in that method was used, an indication of that rule, as
      approved, and of the rule it replaced; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for a boiler or heater for which the determination
    by means of a CEMS test of its NO~x~ emission intensity for a
    compliance test is referred to in subparagraph 38(2)(a)(ii),
    paragraph 38(2)(b) or subparagraph 38(2)(c)(ii) of these
    Regulations,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for each averaging period in the reference
      period,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the day and hour when the averaging period
        began,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the number of hours in the averaging period,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} confirmation that at least 50% of the input
        energy in the boiler's or heater's combustion chamber resulted
        from the introduction of gaseous fossil fuel for each of those
        hours,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} for modern boilers or heaters, an indication as
        to whether the type of gaseous fossil fuel that was combusted
        was natural gas or alternative gas, and

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the NO~x~ emission intensity, beingthe greatest
        of the rolling hourly averages that were determined for the
        NO~x~ emission intensity,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether the EC CEMS Code or
      the Alberta CEMS Code was used and, if an alternative rule as
      approved under subsection 113(1) of these Regulations to replace a
      rule set out in that CEMS Reference Method was used, an indication
      of that rule, as approved, and of the rule it replaced, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for a determination referred to in subparagraph
      38(2)(a)(ii), the name of the manufacturer, along with the serial
      number, make and model and the identifier, if any, within the
      facility where the other boiler or heater --- on which the CEMS
      test referred to in paragraph 26(2)(b) was conducted --- is
      located.

## [SCHEDULE 8]{.scheduleLabel}[(Sections 45 and 47 )]{.OriginatingRef}[Non-application (Engines) --- Information Required]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-830157 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** The following information respecting the responsible person:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} their name, civic and postal addresses, if any,
    telephone number and email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses, if
    any, telephone number and email address of their authorized
    official;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses, if
    any, telephone number and email address of a contact person, if
    different from the authorized official;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name, civic and postal addresses, if any,
    telephone number and email address of each affiliate, if any, of the
    responsible person mentioned in paragraph (a); and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} their gross revenue, and the gross revenue of each
    of those affiliates, for the most recent taxation year for which
    each of them has filed a return of income.

- **2** The following information respecting each of the responsible
  person's pre-existing engines:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if its rated brake power is less than 250 kW, its
    rated brake power, expressed in kW; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if its rated brake power is at least 250 kW,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} its rated brake power, expressed in kW, and its
      make , model and serial number,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type of emission control system, if any,
      with which it is equipped,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the facility where the
      engine is located or, if there is no civic address, its latitude
      and longitude, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} for each of its responsible persons, other than
      the responsible person referred to in paragraph 1(a), if any,
      their name and civic address.
::::
:::::

::::: section
:::: {#830172 .Schedule}
::: {}
## [SCHEDULE 9]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 45, paragraphs 64(4)(b) and 94(3)(a) and subsection 97(6))]{.OriginatingRef}[Engine Registry --- Information Required]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-830173 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** The following information respecting a responsible person who
  registers an engine in the engine registry, applies for a unique
  alphanumeric identifier for an engine or replaces a pre-existing
  engine in their group with a replacement unit:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} their name, civic and postal addresses, if any,
    telephone number and email address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses, if
    any, telephone number and email address of their authorized
    official;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses, if
    any, telephone number and email address of a contact person, if
    different from the authorized official;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether they made an election
    under subsection 61(1) of these Regulations and, if so, the date on
    which they made the election; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether they revoked their
    election under subsection 62(1) of these Regulations and, if so, the
    date on which a notice of revocation was provided.

- **2** The following information respecting the facility at which the
  engine or replacement unit is located:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the facility or, if there is
    no civic address, its latitude and longitude;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} its National Pollutant Release Inventory
    identification number assigned by the Minister for the purpose of
    section 48 of the Act and its provincial identification number, if
    any;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for a modern engine, an indication as to which
    paragraph of subsection 46(4) of these Regulations describes; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for a pre-existing engine, an indication that the
    facility is an oil and gas facility other than an asphalt refinery.

- **3** The following information respecting the engine or replacement
  unit:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether the responsible person
    referred to in section 1 is its owner or operator, or both;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if that responsible person became an owner of the
    engine on a date that is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on or after the day on which these Regulations
      are registered, that date, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} before the day on which these Regulations are
      registered, an indication to that effect;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for each of its responsible persons, other than the
    responsible person referred to in section 1, if any,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} their name and civic address, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether they are its owner
      or operator, or both;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} its serial number or if, the serial number is not
    known or cannot be obtained, its unique alphanumeric identifier;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} its make and model;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an engine, an indication as to
    whether it is a pre-existing engine or modern engine;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an engine, an indication as to
    whether it is low-use or regular-use;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} its rated brake power, expressed in kW;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an engine, an indication as to
    whether it is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a two-stroke lean-burn engine,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a two-stroke rich-burn engine,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a four-stroke lean-burn engine, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a four-stroke rich-burn engine;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the type of emission control system, if any, with
    which it is equipped and, if it was so equipped on a date after
    March 31, 2020, that date;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a modern engine, the date on which
    it began to operate;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a pre-existing engine,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which it was designated as belonging
      to the responsible person's group, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the date on which that
      pre-existing engine ceased to belong to the responsible person's
      group;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a replacement unit,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the serial number --- or, if the serial number is
      not known or cannot be obtained, the unique alphanumeric
      identifier --- of the pre-existing engine that it replaced,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date on which that pre-existing engine
      ceased to belong to the responsible person's group,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the number of hours that that pre-existing
      engine operated while belonging to the group during the 36 months
      preceding that date, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether it is a modern
      engine, an electric motor or a combustion turbine;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} if the group of engines of the responsible person
    referred to in section 1 is subject to section 59 of these
    Regulations, an indication that the engine belongs to the subset
    referred to in that section; and

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} if the responsible person referred to in section 1
    is subject to a limit referred to in section 60 of these
    Regulations,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the identifier of the subgroup to which the
      engine or replacement unit belongs and the date on which it was
      designated as so belonging,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for an engine that has a default NO~x~ emission
      value referred to in subparagraph 66(2)(a)(i) of these
      Regulations, an indication as to whether clauses 66(2)(a)(i)(A)
      to (C) are complied with, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for an engine that has a NO~x~ emission value
      assigned under subsection 67(1) of these Regulations that is
      different from its default NO~x~ emission value set out in
      subsection 66(2) of these Regulations,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the NO~x~ emission value that is assigned to
        it, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} an indication that

        - [(I)]{.lawlabel} the assigned NO~x~ emission value is greater
          than the NO~x~ emission intensity for the engine that was
          determined by means of the most recent performance test
          conducted on it, and

        - [(II)]{.lawlabel} the responsible person has a record of the
          information set out in subparagraphs 100(i)(i) to (iii) of
          these Regulations and section 6 of Schedule 10 in respect of
          that most recent performance test.

- **4** The following information respecting each subgroup that the
  responsible person established under section 65 of these Regulations:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its identifier; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the units, either ppmvd~15%~ or g/kWh, that are
    used to express the NO~x~ emission value of the pre-existing engines
    and replacement units belonging to that subgroup.
::::
:::::

::::: section
:::: {#830226 .Schedule}
::: {}
## [SCHEDULE 10]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 45, clause 66(2)(a)(i)(B), subparagraphs 77(c)(i) and (d)(ii), subsection 99(1) and subclause 3(o)(iii)(B)(II) of Schedule 9)]{.OriginatingRef}[Compliance Report (Engines) --- Information Required]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-830227 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** The name and telephone number of the responsible person who is
  providing the compliance report.

- **2** For each of the responsible person's engines, the total number
  of hours, if any, contained in all periods referred to in section 49
  of these Regulations in the preceding year for which the compliance
  report is provided.

- **3** The following information respecting each of the responsible
  person's low-use engines:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the serial number --- or, if the serial number is
    not known or cannot be obtained, the unique alphanumeric identifier
    --- of the engine;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of hours that the engine operated during
    the preceding year for which the compliance report is provided,
    excluding any hours of operation during an emergency, based on any
    recordings referred to in paragraph 51(2)(c) or (d) of these
    Regulations and, if applicable, paragraph 51(1)(a) of these
    Regulations; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the number of hours that the engine
    operated during an emergency during that preceding year.

- **4** If applicable, the following information respecting the subset
  referred to in section 59 of these Regulations:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for each engine belonging to the subset and each
    engine referred to in subsection 59(2) of these Regulations whose
    rated brake power is included in the subset's or group's total rated
    brake power, its serial number or, if the serial number is not known
    or cannot be obtained, its unique alphanumeric identifier; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total rated brake power of the subset,
    expressed as a percentage of the total rated brake power of the
    responsible person's group.

- **5** The following information respecting each of the responsible
  person's subgroups, if they made an election under subsection 61(1) of
  these Regulations that is in effect for the preceding year for which
  the compliance report is provided:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the serial number --- or, if the serial number is
    not known or cannot be obtained, the unique alphanumeric identifier
    --- of the engines and replacement units that belonged to the
    subgroup during that preceding year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for each of those engines and replacement units,
    each NO~x~ emission value that was assigned to it during that
    preceding year;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for each of those engines and replacement units and
    for each NO~x~ emission value that was assigned to it during that
    preceding year, the number of hours referred to in the description
    of T~ij~ in subsection 61(2) of these Regulations; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the yearly average NO~x~ emission intensity of the
    subgroup, as determined in accordance with subsection 61(2) of these
    Regulations, expressed in ppmvd~15%~ or g/kWh.

- **6** The following information respecting each performance test
  referred to in clause 66(2)(a)(i)(B) or section 77, 78, 92 or 93 of
  these Regulations that was conducted on an engine by the responsible
  person during the preceding year for which the compliance report is
  provided:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person and of the individual, if
    different, who conducted the performance test and the date on which
    it was conducted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the serial number --- or, if the serial number is
    not known or cannot be obtained, the unique alphanumeric identifier
    --- of the engine;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the method set out in each of subsections 72(1)
    to (4) of these Regulations that was used to conduct the test and,
    if an alternative rule as approved under subsection 113(1) of these
    Regulations to replace a rule set out in that method was used, an
    indication of that rule, as approved, and of the rule it replaced;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the lowest concentration of O~2~ in the engine's
    exhaust gas --- from among the three test runs --- as determined in
    accordance with subsection 72(2) of these Regulations; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the NO~x~ emission intensity of the engine, as
    determined in accordance with section 75 of these Regulations.

- **7** The following information respecting each engine on which an
  emissions check referred to in section 79, 92 or 93 of these
  Regulations was conducted by the responsible person during the
  preceding year for which the compliance report is provided:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a rich-burn engine, an indication as to
    whether, for each 90-day period referred to in subparagraph 78(b)(i)
    of these Regulations, there is at least one determination by an
    emissions check under section 89 of these Regulations of the
    concentration of NO~x~ in the exhaust gas that does not exceed the
    NO~x~ emission intensity limit, expressed in ppmvd~15%~, that
    applies to the engine; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a lean-burn engine, for at least one emissions
    check during each 365-day period referred to in paragraph 79(a),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the emissions check was
      conducted,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the serial number --- or, if the serial number
      is not known or cannot be obtained, the unique alphanumeric
      identifier --- of the engine,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the concentration of O~2~ in the exhaust gas of
      the engine referred to in subsection 89(1) of these Regulations,
      and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether the concentration of
      NO~x~ in the exhaust gas of the engine, as determined by the
      emissions check, does not exceed the NO~x~ emission intensity
      limit, expressed in ppmvd~15%~, that applies to the engine.
::::

::: {}
## [SCHEDULE 11]{.scheduleLabel}[(Sections 101 and 108)]{.OriginatingRef}[Compliance Report (Cement Manufacturing Facilities) --- Information Required]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-830257 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** The following information respecting the responsible person:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether they are an owner or
    operator, or both, of the cement manufacturing facility and their
    name and civic address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses,
    telephone number and email address of their authorized official; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses,
    telephone number and email address of a contact person, if different
    from the authorized official.

- **2** The following information respecting the cement manufacturing
  facility:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for each of its responsible persons, other than the
    responsible person referrred to in paragraph 1(a),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} their name and civic address, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether they are an owner or
      operator, or both;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} its name and civic address, if any;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} its latitude and longitude;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} its National Pollutant Release Inventory
    identification number assigned by the Minister for the purpose of
    section 48 of the Act;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the number of kilns;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} for each kiln, information that identifies it;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} for each kiln, its type, that is, whether it is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a precalciner kiln,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a preheater kiln,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a wet kiln, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a long dry kiln; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} for each kiln, for the preceding year for which the
    compliance report is provided,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of NO~x~ emitted, expressed in kg,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of SO~2~ emitted, expressed in kg,
      and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of clinker produced, expressed in
      t.

- **3** If applicable, the emission intensity elected by the responsible
  person under subsection 104(2) of these Regulations as the value for
  EI~NOxi~ in subsection 104(1) of these Regulations.
::::
:::::

::::: section
:::: {#830282 .Schedule}
::: {}
## [SCHEDULE 12]{.scheduleLabel}[(Subsection 110(2))]{.OriginatingRef}[Auditor's Report --- Information Required]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-830283 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** The name, civic address and telephone number of the responsible
  person.

- **2** For each CEMS used to determine under Part 1 a NO~x~ emission
  intensity of one or more boilers or heaters located in a facility or
  under section 106 a quantity of emissions of one or more kilns located
  in a cement manufacturing facility,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the identifier of each of those boilers or heaters
    within the facility where it is located or of each of those kilns
    within the cement manufacturing facility where it is located,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the following information respecting that facility
    or cement manufacturing facility:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} its civic address or, if there is no civic
      address, its latitude and longitude

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} its National Pollutant Release Inventory
      identification number assigned by the Minister for the purpose of
      section 48 of the Act and, in the case of that facility, its
      provincial identification number, if any; and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a facility where a boiler or
      heater is located, an indication as to which paragraph of
      subsection 5(2) of these Regulations describes it.

- **3** The name, civic address, telephone number and email address of
  the auditor and their qualifications.

- **4** The dates on which the audit was conducted.

- **5** The procedures that the auditor followed in assessing whether

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the responsible person's use of the CEMS has
    complied with, as the case may be,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the Quality Assurance/Quality Control manual
      referred to in Section 6.0 of the EC CEMS Code, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the Quality Assurance Plan referred to in
      Section 5.1 of the Alberta CEMS Code;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the responsible person has complied with the CEMS
    Reference Method; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the CEMS has met the specifications set out in the
    CEMS Reference Method and, in particular, that the results of each
    relative accuracy test and each bias test for each of the relative
    accuracy test audits (RATA) for the 12 months preceding the
    auditor's audit have not exceeded the applicable limit referred to
    in the CEMS Reference Method.

- **6** A statement of the auditor's opinion as to whether the
  responsible person has complied with the CEMS Reference Method and, in
  particular, as to whether

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the CEMS has met the specifications set out in the
    CEMS Reference Method;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the results of each relative accuracy test and each
    bias test for each of the relative accuracy test audits (RATA) for
    the 12 months preceding the auditor's audit have not exceeded the
    applicable limit referred to in the CEMS Reference Method; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the responsible person has established a Quality
    Assurance/Quality Control manual that complies with Section 6.0 of
    the EC CEMS Code or a Quality Assurance Plan that complies with
    Section 5.1 of the Alberta CEMS Code and, if so, whether

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the responsible person has implemented it,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} its implementation ensures that the data that
      was generated by the CEMS is complete, accurate and precise, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} it has been updated in accordance with the CEMS
      Reference Method.

- **7** Any recommendation of the auditor for improvements in the CEMS
  or its operation.

## AMENDMENTS NOT IN FORCE {#amendments-not-in-force .scheduleTitleText}

- --- SOR/2016-151, s. 124

  - ###### Section 11 {#section-11 .MarginalNote}

    [124]{.lawlabel} Section 11 of these Regulations is replaced by the
    following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Class 80 and class 70 {#class-80-and-class-70 .MarginalNote}

      [11]{.lawlabel} The NO~x~ emission intensity of a pre-existing
      boiler or heater that is class 80 or class 70 --- other than those
      referred to in subsections 13(1) and 14(1) and (2 ) --- and that,
      for a given hour, has at least 50% of the input energy in its
      combustion chamber resulting from the introduction of gaseous
      fossil fuel must not, for that hour, exceed the limit of 26 g/GJ.
    :::
    ::::

- --- SOR/2016-151, s. 125(2)

      :::

      Regulations is replaced by the following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText

- --- SOR/2016-151, s. 133

  - ###### Section 49 {#section-49 .MarginalNote}

    [133]{.lawlabel} Section 49 of these Regulations is replaced by the
    following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Synthetic gas and still gas {#synthetic-gas-and-still-gas .MarginalNote}

      [49]{.lawlabel} Sections 54, 55, 57, 58 and 68 do not apply in
      respect of an engine --- for any period during which the fuel
      combusted consists of more than 50% synthetic gas, still gas or
      any combination of those gases --- if records are kept that
      establish, based on a calculation of the mass flow, that the fuel
      combusted in that period consists of that proportion of those
      gases.
    :::
    ::::

    replaced by the following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Engines belonging to a group {#engines-belonging-to-a-group .MarginalNote}

      [58]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 60, the NO~x~ emission
      intensity of an engine that belongs to a group must not exceed the
      limit of 210 ppmvd~15%~ or 4 g/kWh, whichever applies.
    :::

    [137]{.lawlabel} Section 60 of these Regulations is replaced by the

    - ###### Certain pre-existing engines {#certain-pre-existing-engines .MarginalNote}

      [60]{.lawlabel} A responsible person who makes an election in
      accordance with subsection 61(1) to opt out of the application of
      section 58 must --- for each year that follows the making of the
      election --- ensure that the yearly average NO~x~ emission
      intensity of each subgroup that they establish under section 65
      does not exceed the limit of 210 ppmvd~15%~ or 4 g/kWh, whichever
      applies.
    :::
    ::::
